 2  // Created by shenyq June/19/2019
 3  // 宗门服务入口
 4  // 本模块负责宗门相关逻辑
 5  
 6  // 基本概念
 7  /*
 8  * 宗门是类似帮派的概念
 9  * 玩家可以注册宗门，注销宗门，加入宗门，离开宗门
10  * 本服务只负责宗门的各种增删查改和持久化，不负责具体和游戏逻辑相关的东西
11  * 如：宗门对修炼有加成这类逻辑本服务不提供
12  */
13  
14  // 本模块负责：
15  // 1.启动重启服务
16  // 2.提供对外的宗门操作相关API
17  // 3.维护所有宗门实例并负责持久化

 82  // 启动活动服务
 83  public void start()
 84  {
 85      // 加载ClanImpD
 86      load_static(ClanImpD, domain.create(ClanImpD));
 87      LOAD_DAEMON(UnionD);
 88      LOAD_DAEMON(UnionUserD);
 89      LOAD_DAEMON(UnionSerialExecuteD);
 90  
 91       // 当前服务所在域
 92      _service_domain := this_domain();
 93  
 94       // 注册协程池
 95      //CoroutineD.register_service(CO_SERVICE_NAME, 1);
 96      service_pool=&gt;new_pool(CO_SERVICE_NAME,{"domain" : _service_domain, "co_count" : 1, "warning_threshold" : 300});
 97      _service_co_port := service_pool=&gt;find_pool(CO_SERVICE_NAME);
 98       // 初始化一下service所在的coroutine
 99      handle evt = sync_object.create_event();
100      _service_co_port.dispatch_service((){
101          _service_co := this_coroutine();
102  
103          // 创建并加载所有山峰
104          this.create_clans();
105  
106          sort_level_clans();
107  
108          evt.raise();
109      });
110  
111          // 等待山峰创建完毕
112      evt.wait();
113      evt.close();
114  
115      if(is_game_server())
116      {
117          init_refresh_cookies();
118  
119          // 山峰每日数据刷新
120          map times = time.info(CalcD.calc_clan_daily_refresh_time());
121          schedule=&gt;create_scheme_format("refresh_clans", (: refresh_clans :), "day", {"times" : [times]});
122  
123          // 山峰每周数据刷新
124          map week_times = time.info(CalcD.calc_clan_weekly_refresh_time());
125          schedule=&gt;create_scheme_format("week_refresh_clans", (:week_refresh_clans:), "week", {"date" : [week_times.wday], "times" : [week_times]});
126  
127          int clan_refresh_capacity_time = CalcD.get_calc_attrib("clan_refresh_capacity_time") ?? COMBAT_CAPACITY_REFRESH_TIME;
128          // 定时刷新山峰战力值定时器
129          timer.create(clan_refresh_capacity_time, REFRESH_CAPACITY_TIMER, (:refresh_all_clan_combat_capacity:));
130  
131          // 定时保存山峰对象
132          timer.create(CHECK_AUTOSAVE_REFRESH_TIME, CHECK_AUTOSAVE_TIMER, (:auto_save_clans:));
133  
134          m_update_clan_rid_to_names();
135      }
136      
137      load_configs();
138  
139      TRACE_WARN("山峰服务启动成功 co = %O",_service_co);
140  
141      _started := true;
142      hook.invoke("CLAN_START_OK");
143  }

150  void init_refresh_cookies()
151  {
152      int cur_time = time.time();
153      _clan_daily_refresh_cookie := mongo.local_agent("base").get_config("clan_daily_refresh_cookie") ?? 0;
154  
155      _clan_weekly_refresh_cookie := mongo.local_agent("base").get_config("clan_weekly_refresh_cookie") ?? 0;
156  
157      int daily_refresh_time = CalcD.calc_clan_daily_refresh_time();
158  
159      // 山峰初始化的时候，错过了当天的每日数据重置时间，有可能服务器在维护，占用了刷新时间。
160      if(_clan_daily_refresh_cookie &gt; 0  &amp;&amp; cur_time &gt; daily_refresh_time &amp;&amp; _clan_daily_refresh_cookie &lt; daily_refresh_time)
161      {
162          TRACE_WARN("初始化山峰服务时，触发刷新山峰每日数据,_clan_daily_refresh_cookie=%O,daily_refresh_time=%O,cur_time=%O",_clan_daily_refresh_cookie,daily_refresh_time,cur_time);
163          refresh_clans();
164      }
165  
166      // 山峰初始化的时候，错过了当周的每周数据重置时间，有可能服务器在维护，占用了刷新时间。
167      int weekly_refresh_time = CalcD.calc_clan_weekly_refresh_time();
168      if(_clan_weekly_refresh_cookie &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; cur_time &gt; weekly_refresh_time &amp;&amp; _clan_weekly_refresh_cookie &lt; weekly_refresh_time)
169      {
170          TRACE_WARN("初始化山峰服务时，触发刷新山峰每周数据,_clan_weekly_refresh_cookie=%O,weekly_refresh_time=%O,cur_time=%O",_clan_weekly_refresh_cookie,weekly_refresh_time,cur_time);
171          week_refresh_clans();
172      }
173  }

194  public parallel bool check_power(int power, string power_type)

207  public void iterate_all_clan_in_co(function func, ...)
208  {
209      if ($? &gt; 1)
210          func.append_arg(unpack(1));
211  
212      dispatch((){
213          for (mixed __id, object clan_ob : _clans)
214              func.call(clan_ob);

218  public void iterate_all_clan_member_in_co(mixed clan_rid, function func, ...)
219  {
220      if ($? &gt; 2)
221          func.append_arg(unpack(2));
222  
223      dispatch((){
224          object clan_ob = is_string(clan_rid) ? get_clan(clan_rid) : clan_rid;
225          if (!clan_ob)
226              return;
227          
228          array members = clan_ob.get_members().keys();
229  
230          for (string member : members)
231              if (!clan_ob.is_npc_member(member))
232                  func.call(member);
233      });
234  }

236  // 抛到服务所在co执行
237  public parallel void dispatch(function func, ...)
238  {
239      if(func.get_arg_domain() == nil)
240      {
241          //绑定山峰参数域
242          func.bind_arg_domain(_service_domain);
243      }
244      // 必须是在clan域中执行
245      assert(func.get_arg_domain() == _service_domain, "clan域：%O，函数参数域：%O", _service_domain, func.get_arg_domain());
246      if ($? &gt; 1)
247          func = func.dup().append_dup_arg_arr(($&lt;)[1..&lt;1]);
248      _service_co_port.dispatch_service(func);
249  }

257  
258      // func 与 evt需要传参传递，否则会此闭包参数域会绑定执行时所在的域
259      dispatch((mixed evt, mixed func) {
260          func.call();

335  // 创建山峰对象
336  public object new_clan(map dbase)
337  {
338      // 使用默认数据创建
339      map d = ClanImpD.get_default_clan_data().deep_dup();
340      d &lt;&lt; dbase;
341      //山峰创建时间
342      d["create_time"] = time.time();
343      object clan_ob = create_clan(d);
344      if(!clan_ob)
345      {
346          TRACE_ERROR("clan:new_clan failed, dbase=%O",d);
347          return nil;
348      }
349      clan_ob.refresh();
350  
351      dispatch((: sort_level_clans :));
352  
353      m_update_clan_rid_to_names();
354      // m_update_clan_rid_to_gangs();
355      return clan_ob;
356  }

358  // 创建山峰对象（可能是从数据库中读取）
359  public object create_clan(map dbase)
360  {
361      //GM会调用这个接口，不是在山峰协程
362      if(!IS_DEBUG)
363          assure_in_clan_co();
364      assert(dbase.rid);
365      assert(dbase.name);
366      assert(dbase.gang_id);
367      if (get_clan_by_name(dbase.name))
368      {
369          TRACE_WARN("山峰名字(%s)重复", dbase.name);
370          return nil;
371      }
372      object ob = new_object(ClanOb, _service_domain, dbase);
373      TRACE_INFO("创建:%s", ob.desc());
374      _clan_name_dict[dbase.name] = ob;
375      _clan_client_syn_info[dbase.rid] = ob.get_client_info();
376      _clans[dbase.rid] = ob;
377      _clans_ob := _clans;
378      return ob;
379  }
380  
381  // 销毁山峰对象
382  public void destruct_clan(string rid)
383  {
384      assure_in_clan_co();
385  
386      object ob = _clans[rid];
387      assert(ob, "山峰%s不存在", rid);
388      TRACE_INFO("销毁:%s", ob.desc());
389      _clans.delete(rid);
390      _clans_ob := _clans;
391      _clan_name_dict.delete(ob.query("name"));
392      _clan_client_syn_info.delete(rid);
393      broadcast_modify_clan_info(rid, nil);
394      // 只打dropped标记，不真的从数据库删除
395      ob.set("dropped", true);
396  
397      // 在coroutine中保存并删除
398      co_pool_execute_in(ob, (){
399          ob.save();
400          ob.close();
401      });
402  
403      dispatch((: sort_level_clans :));
404  
405      m_update_clan_rid_to_names();
406      // m_update_clan_rid_to_gangs();
407  }
408  
409  // 加载并创建所有山峰
410  public void create_clans()
411  {
412      if(!is_game_server())
413          return;
414      map union_names = {};
415      object mongo_agent = mongo.local_agent("custom");
416      array clans = mongo_agent.find_clans({"dropped":nil}, {"_id" : 0});
417      for (map dbase : clans)
418      {
419          object clan_ob = create_clan(dbase);
420          if(clan_ob &amp;&amp; clan_ob.is_union() &amp;&amp; clan_ob.is_master_clan() &amp;&amp; clan_ob.get_union_name())
421          {
422              union_names[clan_ob.get_union_name()] = true;
423          }
424          catch(clan_ob.refresh_clan_union_active_flag());
425      }
426  
427      UnionD.set_local_local_names(union_names);
428  }

806  // 获取战力排名前n的宗门
807  public array get_top_clan_infos(int top, int min_member_count = 0, bool refresh_cc = false)
808  {
809      // 宗门按照战力排序
810      array clan_obs = _clans.values();
811  
812      // 筛选一下成员数量不足min_member_count = 宗门
813      clan_obs = clan_obs.filter((object ob){return ob.get_member_num() &gt;= min_member_count});
814  
815      if(refresh_cc)
816      {
817          for(object ob : clan_obs)
818          {
819              ob.refresh_combat_capacity();
820          }
821      }
822  
823      clan_obs = clan_obs.sort((object left_ob, object right_ob){
824          return left_ob.get_combat_capacity() &gt; right_ob.get_combat_capacity();
825      });
826  
827      int count = math.min(clan_obs.length(), top);
828  
829      array clan_list = [];
830      for(int i = 0; i &lt; count; i++)
831      {
832          object clan_ob = clan_obs[i];
833          map info = {};
834          info.rid = clan_ob.get_rid();
835          info.name = clan_ob.get_name();
836          info.cc = clan_ob.get_combat_capacity();
837          info.member_count = clan_ob.get_member_num();
838          info.server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
839          clan_list.push_back(info);
840      }
841      return clan_list;
842  }

844  // 查找玩家所在山峰对象的rid和power
845  public array get_clan_rid_and_power(string user_rid)
846  {
847      if(!_clan_member_dict)
848          return [nil, 0];
849      mixed clan_rid = _clan_member_dict[user_rid];
850      if(clan_rid == nil)
851          return [nil, 0];
852      object clan_ob = get_clan(clan_rid);
853      if(!clan_ob)
854          return [nil, 0];
855      int power = clan_ob.get_member_value(user_rid, "power") ?? 0;
856      return [clan_rid, power];
857  }

 42  readonly array _union_score_rank_config;

 45  
 46  readonly int _calc_star_times := -1;
 47

 61  public override void FDaemonBase.on_create()
 62  {
 63      hook.register("CLAN_UNION_VICE_OWNER_CHANGE", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_clan_vice_owner_change :));
 64      hook.register("ADD_CLAN_MEMBER", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_clan_add_member :));
 65      hook.register("POST_REMOVE_CLAN_MEMBER", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_clan_remove_member :));
 66      hook.register("USER_RENAME", __PURE_FILE__, (:on_new_rename:));
 67      hook.register("CLAN_LEVEL_UP", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_clan_level_up :));
 68      hook.register("CLAN_BUILD_HERITAGE_LEVEL_UP", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_clan_reward_level_up :));
 69      hook.register("UNION_DISMISS", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_union_dismiss_hook :));
 70      hook.register("UNION_JOIN", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_union_join_hook :));
 71      hook.register("UNION_CREATE", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_union_create_hook :));
 72      hook.register("UNION_ADD_CLAN_MEMBER", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_union_add_clan_member_hook :));
 73      hook.register("UNION_MEMBER_DISMISS", __PURE_FILE__, (: on_union_member_dismiss_hook :));
 74      // 报名阶段
 75      hook.register("GANG_SEASON_DO_SIGN", __PURE_FILE__, (:on_do_sign:));
 76      hook.register("GANG_SEASON_GAME_STOP", __PURE_FILE__, (:on_game_stop:));
 77      hook.register("GANG_SEASON_DO_SEASON_END", __PURE_FILE__, (:on_season_stop:));
 78  
 79      function.call_in_domain(schedule.get_domain(), (){
 80          // 检查宗门星级是否触发计算
 81          map times = time.info(calc_sign_check_time());
 82          if(schedule.get_task("union_check_clan_calc_star_1"))
 83              schedule.delete_task("union_check_clan_calc_star_1");
 84          schedule.create_scheme_format("union_check_clan_calc_star", (: union_check_clan_calc_star :), "day", {"times" : [times]});
 85  
 86          map union_check_times = time.info(calc_union_check_time());
 87          if(schedule.get_task("union_check_clan_union_valid_1"))
 88              schedule.delete_task("union_check_clan_union_valid_1");
 89          schedule.create_scheme_format("union_check_clan_union_valid", (: all_union_clan_check_valid :), "day", {"times" : [union_check_times]});
 90      });

 97  // 加载配置
 98  public void load_configs()
 99  {
100      load_xlsx_config([ "gang_union_config.xlsx", "gang_union_power.xlsx", "gang_union_score.xlsx", "gang_union_score_rank.xlsx"], (: on_load_xlsx_configs :));
101  }
102  
103  map on_load_xlsx_configs(map result_dict)
104  {
105      _config := result_dict["gang_union_config.xlsx"];
106      _power_config := result_dict["gang_union_power.xlsx"];
107      for(int power_id : _power_config.keys())
108      {
109          if(power_id &gt;= UNION_POWER.MAX || power_id &lt;= UNION_POWER.NONE)
110              assert(false,"error_union_power");
111      }
112      _union_score_config := result_dict["gang_union_score.xlsx"];
113      int max_range_rank = 0;
114      int last_range_rank = 0;
115      for(map info : _union_score_config)
116      {
117          if(info.rank_range[0] &gt; max_range_rank)
118              max_range_rank = info.rank_range[0];
119          if(info.rank_range[1] &gt;last_range_rank)
120              last_range_rank = info.rank_range[1];
121      }
122      MAX_RANGE_RANK := max_range_rank;
123      LAST_RANGE_RANK := last_range_rank;
124      _union_score_rank_config := result_dict["gang_union_score_rank.xlsx"];
125      return result_dict;
126  }
127  
128  public mixed get_config(string key)
129  {
130      map config_data = _config[key];
131      if(config_data)
132          return config_data.value;
133      return nil;
134  }
135  
136  // 根据玩家排名获取积分
137  public int get_score_by_user_battle_rank(int rank)
138  {
139      int len = _union_score_config.length();
140      for(int i = len-1; i &gt;= 0 ;i --)
141      {
142          map range = _union_score_config[i];
143          if(rank &gt;= range.rank_range[0] &amp;&amp; rank &lt;= range.rank_range[1])
144              return range.score;
145      }
146      return _union_score_config[len -1].score;
147  }
148  
149  public int get_clan_star_by_score(int score)
150  {
151      int max_score = 0;
152      for(map info : _union_score_rank_config)
153      {
154          if(info.rank_range[1] &gt; max_score)
155              max_score = info.rank_range[1];
156          if(score &gt;= info.rank_range[0] &amp;&amp; score &lt;= info.rank_range[1])
157              return info.score;
158      }
159      if(score &gt; max_score)
160          return _union_score_rank_config[_union_score_rank_config.length() - 1].score;
161      return _union_score_rank_config[0].score;
162  }
163  
164  public int get_max_range_rank()

166      return MAX_RANGE_RANK - 1;
167  }

215  {

217      if(!data)
218      {
219          TRACE_ERROR("获取仙盟权限错误,data == nil power=%O, power_type=%O", power, power_type);

227  public object get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(string union_rid)

338  // 是否是合法的结义名字
339  public array is_valid_union_name(object user, string name, bool check_len = true)
340  {
341      if(name == nil || name == "")
342          return [false, TT("uniond_69303")];
343  
344      if (RiskManageD.get_close_user_define_text())
345      {
346          return [false, TT("uniond_5715")];
347      }
348  
349      if (name == TT("T_daoyuan"))
350          return [false, TT("uniond_69303")];
351  
352  
353      if(check_len)
354      {
355          int max_name_len = get_config("max_name_len") ?? 4;
356          //bool is_chinese_server = PublicServerD.is_cn_or_no_public_server();
357          let int len, int en, int ch = utf8_len(name);
358          if ( ch &gt; max_name_len || en &gt; 0 || ch &lt;= 0)
359              return [false, TT("uniond_67661")];
360      }
361  
362      if(is_exist_union_name(name))
363      {
364          return [false, TT("uniond_16480")];
365      }
366  
367      let bool success, mixed result = GameSensitiveWordD.check_content_sdk("nickname", name, user);
368      if(!success)
369      {
370          //敏感词检查
371          return [false, TT("uniond_50018")];
372      }
373  
374      if(GameSensitiveWordD.contain_special_char(name))
375      {
376          return [false, TT("uniond_50934")];
377      }
378  
379      let bool speak_success, mixed speak_result = SocialClientD.check_can_speak_and_tip(user);
380      if(!speak_success)
381          return [false, TT("uniond_45097")];
382  
383      return [true,nil];
384  }
385  
386  // 是否是合法的宣言
387  public array is_valid_union_info(object user, string info)
388  {
389      if(info == nil || info == "")
390          return [false, nil];
391  
392      if (RiskManageD.get_close_user_define_text())
393      {
394          return [false, TT("uniond_5715")];
395      }
396  
397      int max_name_len = get_config("union_announce_max_words");
398      //bool is_chinese_server = PublicServerD.is_cn_or_no_public_server();
399      let int len, int en, int ch = utf8_len(info);
400      if ( len &gt; max_name_len)
401          return [false, TT("uniond_13527")];
402  
403      let bool success, mixed result = GameSensitiveWordD.check_content_sdk("text", info, user);
404      if(!success)
405      {
406          //敏感词检查
407          return [false, TT("uniond_27104")];
408      }
409  
410      let bool speak_success, mixed speak_result = SocialClientD.check_can_speak_and_tip(user);
411      if(!speak_success)
412          return [false, TT("uniond_45097")];
413  
414      return [true,nil];
415  }

417  // 创建仙盟
418  public void create_union(string union_owner, string union_owner_name, string name, map clan_info, object clan_ob, bool auto_create = false)
419  {
420      if (this_domain() != clan_ob.get_domain())
421          return function.call_in_domain(clan_ob.get_domain(), (: create_union :), unpack($&lt;));
422      string this_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
423       // 新建宗门
424      string union_rid = RidD.new_rid("UN");
425      map context = {
426          "union_name" : name,     // 仙盟名
427          "union_rid" : union_rid,    // 仙盟rid
428          "union_owner" : union_owner, // 仙盟盟主rid
429          "union_owner_name" : union_owner_name,   // 仙盟盟主名字
430          "union_server_id" : this_server_id, // 仙盟服务器id
431          "union_master_clan_rid" : clan_info.clan_rid,   // 主宗门
432          "members" : {},         // 宗门成员
433          "union_combat_capacity" : clan_ob.get_combat_capacity(),    // 总战力
434          "union_member_count" : clan_ob.get_member_num(),        // 总成员数量
435          "union_info" : "",                             // 宣言
436          "union_create_time" : time.time(),
437          "union_vice_owner_num" : 0,         // 副盟主数量
438          "union_last_change_name_time" : 0,  // 最后改名时间
439          "union_last_change_info_mail_time" : 0,  // 最后修改公告时间
440          "union_change_info_times"   : 0,    // 每日群发公告内容次数
441      };
442      context.members[clan_info.clan_rid] = clan_info;
443      // 注册新的仙盟名称
444      register_union_name(name, this_server_id);
445      // 设置为盟主
446      clan_ob.refresh_member_data(union_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER}, false);
447      clan_ob.new_union_data(context);
448      clan_ob.save();
449      board_clan_members_refresh_clan_data(clan_ob);
450       // 服务器也加入到聊天频道内，否则发不了系统消息
451      add_chat_channel_member(union_rid, "root", true);
452      int auto_flag = auto_create ? 1:0;
453      logex.union_create_log(union_owner, clan_info.clan_rid, clan_ob.get_name(), get_match_servers(), SystemD.get_server_id(),clan_ob.get_star(), union_owner, union_owner_name,union_rid,name,auto_flag);
454      logex.union_duty_change_log("system", clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_ob.get_union_name(),clan_ob.gen_clan_member_log_desc(),union_owner, clan_info.clan_rid, 0, (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER, 2);
455  }

460          map ret = {};

483          return ret;
484      });

506  {

508      string this_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();

512          {
513              union_list &lt;&lt; get_union_list();

515          else

522              }
523  
524              union_list &lt;&lt; ret[0];
525          }
526      }
527  
528      return union_list;

537  // 获取本服的仙盟列表
538  public map get_union_list()
539  {
540      // 实现方式待优化
541      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
542          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: get_union_list :), unpack($&lt;));
543      map union_dict = {};
544      for(string __clan_rid, object clan_ob : clan.get_clans())
545      {
546          if(!clan_ob.is_union() || !clan_ob.is_master_clan())
547              continue;

549          if(union_dict[union_rid])
550              continue;
551          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
552          map union_info = {
553              "union_name" : clan_ob.get_union_name(),
554              "union_rid" : clan_ob.get_union_rid(),
555              "union_server_id" : clan_ob.get_union_server_id(),
556              "union_star"    : clan_ob.get_union_star(),
557              "union_combat_capacity" : union_data.union_combat_capacity ?? 0,
558          };
559          union_dict[union_rid] = union_info;
560      }
561      return union_dict;
562  }

608  // join_type : 1:审批加入
609  public map clan_join_union(object union_clan_ob, map clan_info, int join_type = 0)
610  {
611      // 如果当前是主宗门
612      if(union_clan_ob.is_master_clan())
613      {
614          return clan_join_union_imp(union_clan_ob, clan_info, join_type);
615      }
616      else
617      {
618          string this_server_id =  SystemD.get_server_id();
619          string master_server_id = union_clan_ob.get_union_server_id();
620          // 如果主宗门在本服
621          if(master_server_id == this_server_id)
622          {
623              object master_clan_ob = get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_clan_ob.get_union_rid());
624              if(!master_clan_ob)
625              {
626                  return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_not_exist"};
627              }
628              return clan_join_union_imp(master_clan_ob, clan_info, join_type);
629          }
630          else
631          {
632              // 跨服通知主宗门处理请求
633              master_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(master_server_id);
634              mixed ret = rpc_clan_join_union(master_server_id, union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_info, join_type);
635              if(is_array(ret))
636              {
637                  return ret[0];
638              }
639              else
640              {
641                  return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_rpc_error"};
642              }
643          }
644      }
645  }
646  
647  // 只有主宗门才有权限加入新成员
648  public map clan_join_union_imp(object union_clan_ob, map clan_info, int join_type = 0)
649  {
650      if (this_domain() != union_clan_ob.get_domain())
651          return function.call_in_domain(union_clan_ob.get_domain(), (: clan_join_union_imp :), unpack($&lt;));
652      if(!union_clan_ob.is_union() || !union_clan_ob.is_master_clan())
653      {
654          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_error_state"};
655      }
656  
657      //rpc获取clan的加入信息
658      map ret = get_clan_join_info(union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_clan_ob.get_union_star(), clan_info);
659      if(!ret.ok)
660          return ret;
661      map join_data = ret.join_data;
662  
663      if(!union_clan_ob.add_new_clan_member(join_data))
664      {
665          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:clan_join_union_imp add_new_clan_member failed, union_rid=%s, join_data=%O", union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), join_data);
666          return {"ok":false};
667      }
668  
669      int union_star = union_clan_ob.get_union_star();
670  
671      // 宗门加入仙盟日志
672      int clan_star = clan_info.star ?? clan_info.clan_star;
673      logex.union_gang_change_log("system", union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_clan_ob.get_union_name(), get_match_servers(), 
674          clan_info.clan_rid, clan_info.clan_name, clan_star, join_type, union_star - clan_star, union_star);
675  
676      // rpc同步仙盟的数据给所有宗门
677      broadcast_union_dbase_to_members(union_clan_ob, clan_info.clan_rid, union_clan_ob.get_rid(), true);
678      return {"ok":true};
679  }

682  {
683      RPC_GET_IN_SERVER;
684      object master_clan_ob = get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_rid);
685      map ret = {};

687      {
688          ret = {"ok":false,"reason":"union_not_exist"};
689      }
690      else
691      {
692          ret = clan_join_union_imp(master_clan_ob, clan_info, join_type);
693      }
694      return [ret];
695  }

697  public void broadcast_union_dbase_to_members(object union_clan_ob, string new_member_rid, string except_clan_rid = nil,bool refresh_members = true)
698  {
699      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
700          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: broadcast_union_dbase_to_members :), unpack($&lt;));
701      map dbase = union_clan_ob.get_union_data();
702      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : dbase.members)
703      {
704          // 主宗门不需要再设置了
705          if(except_clan_rid &amp;&amp; clan_rid == except_clan_rid)
706              continue;
707          string clan_server_id = clan_info.clan_server_id;
708          clan_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(clan_server_id);
709          mixed ret = rpc_set_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(clan_server_id, clan_rid, dbase, new_member_rid == clan_rid,  refresh_members);
710          if(!is_array(ret))
711          {
712              catch(assert(false));
713              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:同步仙盟数据给成员失败！！！！, clan_info=%M, union_clan_ob=%s", clan_info,union_clan_ob.desc());
714          }
715      }
716  }

718  bool set_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(string clan_rid, map union_data, bool is_new_member, bool refresh_members = true)
719  {

721          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: set_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid :), unpack($&lt;));
722      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
723      if(!clan_ob)
724          return false;
725      if(is_new_member)
726      {
727          clan_ob.new_union_data(union_data.deep_dup());
728          clan_ob.delete_temp("lock_union_rid");
729      }
730      else
731      {
732          clan_ob.replace_union_data(union_data.deep_dup());
733      }
734  
735      if(refresh_members)
736          board_clan_members_refresh_clan_data(clan_ob);
737      clan_ob.save();
738      return true;
739  }

795  
796  public map get_clan_join_info_local(string request_union_rid, int union_star, map clan_info)
797  {
798     if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
799          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: get_clan_join_info_local :), unpack($&lt;));
800      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_info.clan_rid);
801      if(!clan_ob)
802      {
803          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_clan_not_exist"};
804      }
805      if(clan_ob.is_union())
806      {
807          return {"ok":false,"reason":TT("uniond_37648")};
808      }
809      if(!is_union_open())
810      {
811          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
812      }
813      int clan_star = clan_ob.get_star();
814      if(union_star + clan_star &gt; get_config("union_max_star"))
815      {
816          return {"ok":false,"reason":TT("uniond_49644")};
817      }
818      // 锁定5分钟超时
819      int lock_time = clan_ob.query_temp("lock_union_rid_time") ?? 0;
820      if(lock_time &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; time.time() - lock_time &gt; 300)
821      {
822          clan_ob.delete_temp("lock_union_rid");
823      }
824  
825      string lock_union_rid = clan_ob.query_temp("lock_union_rid");
826      if(lock_union_rid &amp;&amp; lock_union_rid != request_union_rid)
827      {
828          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:get_clan_join_info_local error clan_ob locked, lock_union_rid=%s, request_union_rid=%s,clan_ob=%s", 
829          lock_union_rid, request_union_rid, clan_ob.desc());
830          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_error_state"};
831      }
832  
833      clan_ob.set_temp("lock_union_rid", request_union_rid);
834      clan_ob.set_temp("lock_union_rid_time", time.time());
835      map join_data = gen_union_clan_info(clan_ob);
836      return {"ok":true,"join_data":join_data};
837  }

839  public void broadcast_union_dbase_field_to_members(object union_clan_ob, string key, bool refresh_members = false)
840  {
841      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
842          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: broadcast_union_dbase_field_to_members :), unpack($&lt;));
843      string this_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
844      map dbase = union_clan_ob.get_union_data();
845      mixed val = dbase[key];
846      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : dbase.members)
847      {
848          // 自己不需要再设置了
849          if(clan_rid == union_clan_ob.get_rid())
850              continue;
851          string clan_server_id = clan_info.clan_server_id;
852          clan_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(clan_server_id);
853          if(clan_server_id == this_server_id)
854          {
855              if(is_map(val) || is_array(val))
856              {
857                  val = val.deep_dup();
858              }
859              set_clan_union_dbase_filed_by_clan_rid(clan_rid, key, val, refresh_members);
860          }
861          else
862          {
863              rpc_set_clan_union_dbase_filed_by_clan_rid(clan_server_id, clan_rid, key, val, refresh_members);
864          }
865      }
866  }

883      clan_ob.save();
884      return true;

1080  
1081  public map remove_union_clan_member(string union_rid, string clan_rid, bool refresh_union_vice_owner = true, bool kick = false)
1082  {
1083      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
1084          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: remove_union_clan_member :), unpack($&lt;));
1085      object union_clan_ob = get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_rid);
1086      if(!union_clan_ob)
1087      {
1088          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:remove_union_clan_member error, union_clan_ob == nil, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s", union_rid, clan_rid);
1089          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_not_exist"};
1090      }
1091      if(!union_clan_ob.is_master_clan())
1092      {
1093          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:remove_union_clan_member error, not_master_clan, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s", union_rid, clan_rid);
1094          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
1095      }
1096      map members = union_clan_ob.get_union_members();
1097      if(!members[clan_rid])
1098      {
1099          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:remove_union_clan_member error, not_union_member, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s", union_rid, clan_rid);
1100          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_error_param"};
1101      }
1102      string member_clan_name = members[clan_rid].clan_name;
1103      //string union_owner = union_clan_ob.query_union_field("union_owner");
1104      //string union_name = union_clan_ob.get_union_name();
1105      //string log_clan_member_desc = union_clan_ob.gen_clan_member_log_desc();
1106      if(union_clan_ob.get_union_master_clan_rid() == clan_rid)
1107      {
1108          if(members.length() == 1)
1109          {
1110              //catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", union_rid, union_name, log_clan_member_desc,union_owner, clan_rid, (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER, 0,  2));
1111              union_clan_ob.union_dismiss(true);
1112          }
1113          else
1114          {
1115              map new_owner_info = get_union_next_owner_clan_info(union_clan_ob);
1116              if(is_empty(new_owner_info))
1117              {
1118                  TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:remove_union_clan_member error, new_owner_info == nil, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s", union_rid, clan_rid);
1119                  return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_error_param"};
1120              }
1121              // 替换新的仙盟主
1122              union_clan_ob.change_owner(new_owner_info);
1123              // 移除自己
1124              string union_clan_rid = union_clan_ob.get_rid();
1125              union_clan_ob.remove_union_clan_member(union_clan_rid, false);
1126              map dbase = union_clan_ob.get_union_data().deep_dup();
1127              union_clan_ob.union_dismiss(false);
1128              unregister_union_name(dbase.union_name, SystemD.get_server_id());
1129              // rpc同步仙盟的数据给所有宗门
1130              string this_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
1131              for(string member_clan_rid, map clan_info : dbase.members)
1132              {
1133                  string clan_server_id = clan_info.clan_server_id;
1134                  clan_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(clan_server_id);
1135                  if(clan_server_id == this_server_id)
1136                  {
1137                      replace_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(member_clan_rid, dbase, true);
1138                  }
1139                  else
1140                  {
1141                      mixed ret = rpc_replace_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(clan_server_id, member_clan_rid, dbase, true);
1142                      if(!is_array(ret))
1143                      {
1144                          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:主宗门退出时, 同步仙盟数据给成员失败！！！！, clan_info=%M, union_clan_ob=%s, ret=%M", clan_info,union_clan_ob.desc(), ret);
1145                      }
1146                  }
1147                  // 通知"exit_union_mail"
1148                  rpc_notify_clan_mail(clan_server_id, member_clan_rid, get_config("exit_union_mail"),[member_clan_name]);
1149              }
1150              //catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", union_rid, union_name, log_clan_member_desc,union_owner, clan_rid, (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER, 0,  2));
1151          }
1152      }
1153      else
1154      {
1155          union_clan_ob.remove_union_clan_member(clan_rid);
1156          // rpc同步仙盟的数据给所有宗门
1157          broadcast_union_dbase_to_members(union_clan_ob, nil, union_clan_ob.get_rid(), true);
1158          if(refresh_union_vice_owner)
1159          {
1160              // 刷新一下副盟主的数量
1161              co_pool_execute((){
1162                  refresh_union_vice_owner_num(union_clan_ob);
1163              });
1164          }
1165  
1166          string mail_id = kick ? get_config("kick_gang_mail") : get_config("exit_union_mail");
1167          for(string member_clan_rid, map clan_info : union_clan_ob.get_union_members())
1168          {
1169              rpc_notify_clan_mail(clan_info.clan_server_id, member_clan_rid, mail_id, [member_clan_name]);
1170          }
1171  
1172      }
1173      return {"ok":true};
1174  }

1177  public map get_union_next_owner_clan_info(object union_clan_ob)
1178  {
1179      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
1180          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: get_union_next_owner_clan_info :), unpack($&lt;));
1181      string master_clan_rid = union_clan_ob.get_union_master_clan_rid();
1182      map members = union_clan_ob.get_union_members();
1183      map sel_vice_owner = nil;
1184      int max_vice_owner_combat_capacity = 0;
1185      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : members)
1186      {
1187          if(clan_rid == master_clan_rid)
1188              continue;
1189          // 每个宗门选出一个战力最高的副盟主且必现是宗主
1190          string clan_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(clan_info.clan_server_id);

1201          {
1202              int combat_capacity = clan_data.max_vice_owner.combat_capacity;
1203              if(combat_capacity &gt; max_vice_owner_combat_capacity)
1204              {
1205                  max_vice_owner_combat_capacity = combat_capacity;
1206                  sel_vice_owner = clan_data.max_vice_owner;
1207              }
1208          }
1209      }
1210  
1211      map next_owner_data = sel_vice_owner;
1212  
1213      return next_owner_data;
1214  }

1391  map cancel_union_vice_owner(string clan_rid, string user_rid)
1392  {
1393      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
1394          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: cancel_union_vice_owner :), unpack($&lt;));
1395      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
1396      if(!clan_ob)

1420      object member_user = RID(user_rid);

1516  public map get_union_clan_members(string union_rid, string union_server_id)
1517  {
1518      union_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(union_server_id);
1519      mixed ret = rpc_get_union_clan_list(union_server_id, union_rid);
1520      if(!is_array(ret))
1521      {
1522          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:get_union_clan_members rpc_get_union_clan_list error, ret=%O, union_rid=%s, union_server_id=%s", ret, union_rid,union_server_id);
1523          return nil;
1524      }
1525  
1526      if(!ret[0].ok)
1527      {
1528          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:get_union_clan_members error, union_rid=%s, union_server_id=%s", ret, union_rid,union_server_id);
1529          return nil;
1530      }
1531  
1532      map clan_list = ret[0].clan_list;
1533      map union_members = {};
1534      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : clan_list)
1535      {
1536          mixed rpc_ret = rpc_get_clan_members(clan_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid);
1537          if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
1538          {
1539              union_members[clan_rid] = {};
1540              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:get_union_clan_members rpc_get_clan_members error, rpc_ret=%O, union_rid=%s, union_server_id=%s, clan_info=%M", rpc_ret, union_rid,union_server_id, clan_info);
1541          }
1542          else
1543          {
1544              map members = rpc_ret[0];
1545              // 补充一个server_id字段
1546              for(string __rid, map member_info : members)
1547                  member_info.server_id = clan_info.clan_server_id;
1548              union_members[clan_rid]  = members;
1549          }
1550      }
1551      return union_members;
1552  }

1612  public void sync_broadcast_union_data_imp(object union_clan_ob, map data)
1613  {
1614      if (this_domain() != union_clan_ob.get_domain())
1615          return function.call_in_domain(union_clan_ob.get_domain(), (: sync_broadcast_union_data_imp :), unpack($&lt;));
1616      if(!union_clan_ob.is_master_clan())
1617          return;
1618      map clan_members = union_clan_ob.get_union_members();
1619      string union_clan_rid = union_clan_ob.get_rid();
1620      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : clan_members)
1621      {
1622          if(clan_rid == union_clan_rid)
1623              continue;
1624          mixed ret = rpc_sync_union_data(clan_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid, data);
1625          if(!is_array(ret))
1626          {
1627              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:rpc_sync_union_data rpc_error, clan_info=%M, data =%M, union_clan_ob=%s", clan_info, data, union_clan_rid);

1639  void sync_union_data(string clan_rid, map data)
1640  {
1641      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
1642          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: sync_union_data :), unpack($&lt;));
1643      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
1644      if(!clan_ob)
1645      {
1646          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:sync_union_data clan_ob == nil, clan_rid=%s, data =%M", clan_rid, data);
1647          return;
1648      }
1649  
1650      if(!clan_ob.is_union())
1651      {
1652          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:sync_union_data clan_ob not union, clan_rid=%s, data =%M", clan_rid, data);
1653          return;
1654      }
1655  
1656      for(string key, mixed val : data)
1657      {
1658          clan_ob.set_union_data_filed(key, val, true, false, false);
1659      }
1660      clan_ob.save();
1661  }

1663  public map appoint_union_owner(object union_clan_ob, string clan_server_id, string clan_rid, string owner_rid)
1664  {
1665      if (this_domain() != union_clan_ob.get_domain())
1666          return function.call_in_domain(union_clan_ob.get_domain(), (: appoint_union_owner :), unpack($&lt;));
1667      assert(union_clan_ob.is_master_clan());
1668      assert(union_clan_ob.get_rid() != clan_rid);
1669      // rpc检查一下clan_rid和owner_rid的合法性
1670      mixed ret = rpc_check_clan_owner(clan_server_id, union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_rid, owner_rid);
1671      if(!is_array(ret))
1672      {
1673          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_rpc_error"};
1674      }
1675  
1676      if(!ret[0].ok)
1677          return ret[0];
1678  
1679      string old_union_owner = union_clan_ob.query_union_field("union_owner");
1680      string old_union_master_clan_rid = union_clan_ob.get_union_master_clan_rid();
1681      // 盟主变成副盟主
1682      union_clan_ob.refresh_member_data(old_union_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER});
1683      map new_owner_info ={};
1684      new_owner_info.server_id = clan_server_id;
1685      new_owner_info.clan_rid = clan_rid;
1686      new_owner_info.rid = owner_rid;
1687      new_owner_info.name = ret[0].owner_name;
1688  
1689      // 替换新的仙盟主
1690      union_clan_ob.change_owner(new_owner_info);
1691      map dbase = union_clan_ob.get_union_data();
1692      unregister_union_name(union_clan_ob.get_union_name(), SystemD.get_server_id());
1693      // rpc同步仙盟的数据给所有宗门
1694      string this_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
1695      for(string member_clan_rid, map clan_info : dbase.members)
1696      {
1697          string server_id = clan_info.clan_server_id;
1698          server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(server_id);
1699          if(server_id == this_server_id)
1700          {
1701              replace_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(member_clan_rid, dbase.deep_dup(), true);
1702          }
1703          else
1704          {
1705              mixed rpc_ret = rpc_replace_clan_union_dbase_by_clan_rid(server_id, member_clan_rid, dbase, true);
1706              if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
1707              {
1708                  TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:禅让盟主时, 同步仙盟数据给成员失败！！！！, clan_info=%M, union_clan_ob=%s, ret=%M", clan_info,union_clan_ob.desc(), rpc_ret);
1709              }
1710          }

1800  // 生成匹配范围内的宗门成员战力榜
1801  public map gen_match_range_battle_rank(array server_ids)
1802  {
1803      array total_players = [];
1804      for(string server_id : server_ids)
1805      {
1806          array players = [];
1807          // 遍历当前战力榜
1808          function func = [&amp;players](string rank_name, int rank, map rank_info){
1809              // 有宗门
1810              if(rank_info.crid)
1811              {
1812                  map info = {};
1813                  info.rid = rank_info.rid;
1814                  info.server_id = server_id;
1815                  info.combat_capacity = rank_info.combat_capacity;
1816                  info.level = rank_info.default_level;
1817                  info.sub_level = rank_info.default_sub_level;
1818                  info.crid = rank_info.crid;
1819                  players.push_back(info);
1820              }
1821          };
1822  
1823          int max_rank = get_max_range_rank();
1824          int step = math.min(400, max_rank);
1825          // 轮询战力榜前2000名
1826          RankD.iterate_rank_template(BATTLE_RANK, func, max_rank, true, step, server_id);
1827  
1828          if(players.length() &gt; 0)
1829              total_players.push_back_n(players);
1830      }
1831  
1832      // 按照战力排序
1833      total_players.sort((:player_sort:));
1834  
1835      // 只保留部分排名.
1836      map player_dict = {};
1837      int last_index = get_max_range_rank() - 1;
1838      int len = total_players.length();
1839      int min_combat_capacity = 0;
1840      for(int i = 0; i &lt;= last_index; i++)
1841      {
1842          if(i &gt;= len)
1843              break;
1844          map player_info = total_players[i];
1845          player_info.rank = i+1;
1846          player_dict[player_info.rid] = player_info;
1847          min_combat_capacity = player_info.combat_capacity;
1848      }
1849  
1850      map rank_info = {};
1851      rank_info.player_dict = player_dict;
1852      // 最后一名的战力值
1853      rank_info.min_combat_capacity = min_combat_capacity;
1854      rank_info.time = time.time();
1855      return rank_info;
1856  }
1857  
1858  // 广播战力排名
1859  public void broadcast_match_range_battle_rank_info(map rank_infos)
1860  {
1861      array server_ids = get_match_servers();
1862      for(string server_id : server_ids)
1863      {
1864          mixed ret = rpc_notify_match_range_battle_rank_info(server_id, rank_infos);
1865          if(!is_array(ret))
1866          {
1867              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:broadcast_match_range_battle_rank_info rpc_error server_id = %s, ret = %s", server_id, ret);
1868          }
1869      }
1870  }
1871  
1872  bool player_sort(map a, map b)
1873  {
1874      if(a.combat_capacity != b.combat_capacity)
1875          return a.combat_capacity &gt; b.combat_capacity;
1876      else if(a.level != b.level)
1877      {
1878          return a.level &gt; b.level;
1879      }
1880      else if(a.sub_level != b.sub_level)
1881      {
1882          return a.sub_level &gt; b.sub_level;
1883      }
1884      else
1885      {
1886          return a.rid &gt; b.rid;
1887      }
1888  }

2011  void on_new_rename(object user, string new_name)
2012  {
2013      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
2014      if(!clan_data)
2015          return;
2016      if(clan_data.union_power == nil || clan_data.union_power ==(int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER)
2017          return;
2018      if(clan_data.union_power == (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER)
2019      {
2020          // 不是宗主
2021          if(clan_data.power != (int)CLAN_POWER.OWNER)
2022              return;
2023      }
2024      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
2025      function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
2026          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
2027          if(!clan_ob || !clan_ob.is_union())
2028              return;
2029          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
2030          if(!union_data)
2031              return;
2032          if(union_data.union_owner == user_rid)
2033          {
2034              union_data.union_owner_name = new_name;
2035              clan_ob.set_dirty(true);
2036              // 同步数据给所有宗门成员
2037              sync_broadcast_union_data(clan_ob, {"union_owner_name" : new_name});
2038          }
2039          for(string __rid, map info : union_data.members)
2040          {
2041              if(info.clan_owner == user_rid)
2042              {
2043                  string union_server_id = clan_ob.get_union_server_id();
2044                  string union_rid = clan_ob.get_union_rid();
2045                  map data = {};
2046                  data.clan_owner_name = new_name;
2047                  sync_clan_member_data(union_server_id, union_rid, clan_ob.get_rid(), data);
2048              }
2049          }
2050      });
2051  }

2284  // region 自动结成仙盟
2285  // 自动结成仙盟
2286  public void auto_make_union(array clans)
2287  {
2288      int max_star = get_config("union_max_star");
2289      array clan_list = clans.deep_dup();
2290      // 按照星级和战力排序
2291      array final_union_info = [];
2292      clan_list.sort( (:clan_sort:) );
2293      TRACE_WARN("UnionD:自动结盟,宗门列表=%O", clan_list);
2294      for(map clan_info : clan_list)
2295      {
2296          map union_info = {};
2297          if(clan_info.select)
2298              continue;
2299          clan_info.select = true;
2300          union_info.master_clan = clan_info;
2301          union_info.clan_list = [clan_info];
2302          union_info.total_star = clan_info.star ?? 1;
2303          for(map info : clan_list)
2304          {
2305              if(info.select)
2306                  continue;
2307              if(union_info.total_star + info.star &lt;= max_star)
2308              {
2309                  union_info.total_star += info.star;
2310                  union_info.clan_list.push_back(info);
2311                  info.select = true;
2312              }
2313          }
2314  
2315          final_union_info.push_back(union_info);
2316      }
2317      TRACE_WARN("UnionD:自动结盟,组合的仙盟列表=%O", final_union_info);
2318      co_pool_execute((array final_union_info){
2319          auto_make_union_imp(final_union_info);
2320      }, final_union_info);
2321  }
2322  
2323  bool clan_sort(map a, map b)
2324  {
2325      if(a.star != b.star)
2326          return a.star &gt; b.star;
2327      return a.combat_capacity &gt; b.combat_capacity;
2328  }
2329  
2330  void auto_make_union_imp(array union_list)
2331  {
2332      for(map union_info : union_list)
2333      {
2334          map master_clan_info = union_info.master_clan;
2335          mixed ret = rpc_notify_clan_create_union(master_clan_info.server_id, union_info);
2336          if(!is_array(ret))
2337          {
2338              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:auto_make_union_imp 自动结盟 rpc_error, ret = %O, union_info=%O", ret, union_info);
2339              continue;
2340          }
2341          if(!ret[0].ok)
2342          {
2343              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:auto_make_union_imp 自动结盟 failed, ret = %O, union_info=%O", ret, union_info);
2344              continue;
2345          }
2346  
2347          array clan_list = [];
2348          for(map clan_info : union_info.clan_list)
2349          {
2350              clan_list.push_back([clan_info.rid, clan_info.server_id, clan_info.name]);
2351          }
2352          TRACE_WARN("UnionD:自动结盟成功, master_clan_rid=[%s/%s], clan_list=%M", master_clan_info.rid, master_clan_info.name, clan_list);
2353      }
2354  }
2355  
2356  public array rpc_notify_clan_create_union(string server_id, map union_info)
2357  {
2358      RPC_GET_IN_SERVER;
2359      return [clan_auto_make_union(union_info)];
2360  }
2361  
2362  // 宗门自动结成仙盟
2363  map clan_auto_make_union(map union_info)
2364  {
2365      map master_clan_info = union_info.master_clan;
2366      string union_name = master_clan_info.name;
2367      string clan_rid  = master_clan_info.rid;
2368      // 判断宗门是否存在，并且是否已经是仙盟对象了
2369      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
2370          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
2371          if(!clan_ob)
2372          {
2373              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:clan_auto_make_union error, clan_ob ==nil, clan_rid=%s", clan_rid);
2374              return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_clan_not_exist"};
2375          }
2376          if(clan_ob.is_union())
2377          {
2378              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:clan_auto_make_union error, is_union, clan_rid=%s", clan_rid);
2379              return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_error_state"};
2380          }
2381  
2382          return {"ok":true, "clan_ob":clan_ob};
2383      });
2384  
2385      if(!ret.ok)
2386          return ret;
2387  
2388      if(is_exist_union_name(union_name))
2389      {
2390          for(int i = 1; i &lt; 10; i++)
2391          {
2392              string new_union_name = sprintf("%s%d", union_name, i);
2393              if(!is_exist_union_name(new_union_name))
2394              {
2395                  union_name = new_union_name;
2396                  break;
2397              }
2398          }
2399      }
2400  
2401      map clan_info = gen_union_clan_info(ret.clan_ob);
2402      // 创建
2403      create_union(clan_info.clan_owner, clan_info.clan_owner_name, union_name, clan_info, ret.clan_ob, true);
2404      object union_clan_ob = ret.clan_ob;
2405      TRACE_WARN("UnionD:clan_auto_make_union 创建仙盟 union_rid=%s, union_name=%s", union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_name);
2406      // 邀请宗门加入
2407      for(map member_info : union_info.clan_list)
2408      {
2409          if(member_info.rid == clan_rid)
2410              continue;
2411          map join_ret = clan_join_union_imp(union_clan_ob, {"clan_rid":member_info.rid,"clan_server_id":member_info.server_id,"clan_star":member_info.star,"clan_name":member_info.name}, 5);
2412          if(!join_ret.ok)
2413          {
2414              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:clan_auto_make_union 仙盟增加宗门成员失败, union_rid=%s, union_name=%s, member_info=%M", union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_name, member_info);
2415              continue;
2416          }
2417          TRACE_WARN("UnionD:clan_auto_make_union 仙盟添加宗门成员成功 union_rid=%s, union_name=%s，clan_rid=%s, clan_name=%s",union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_name, member_info.rid,member_info.name);
2418      }
2419      return {"ok":true};
2420  }

2433  // 解散本服得所有主仙盟，协程内调用
2434  public void dismiss_all_union()
2435  {
2436      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
2437          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: dismiss_all_union :), unpack($&lt;));
2438      TRACE_WARN("UnionD:dismiss_all_union 解散仙盟。");
2439      for(string __clan_rid, object clan_ob : clan.get_clans())
2440      {
2441          // 清空邀请信息
2442          if(clan_ob.query("union_invite_list"))
2443              clan_ob.delete("union_invite_list");
2444          // 清空申请列表
2445          if(clan_ob.query("apply_union_list"))
2446              clan_ob.delete("apply_union_list");
2447          if(!clan_ob.is_union() || !clan_ob.is_master_clan())
2448              continue;
2449          catch(union_formal_dismiss(clan_ob));
2450      }
2451  
2452      array server_ids = get_match_servers();
2453      if(server_ids[0] == SystemD.get_server_id())
2454      {
2455          int delay_sec = 300;
2456          if(IS_DEBUG)
2457              delay_sec = 1;
2458          // 可能解散会有遗漏得异常情况，最后保底收尾执行一次全部回收解散
2459          DelayD.delay_invoke(delay_sec, (){
2460              dismiss_all_union_force();
2461          });
2462      }
2463  }
2464  
2465  // 仙盟退出，正式方式
2466  public bool union_formal_dismiss(object union_clan_ob)
2467  {
2468      if (this_domain() != union_clan_ob.get_domain())
2469          return function.call_in_domain(union_clan_ob.get_domain(), (: union_formal_dismiss :), unpack($&lt;));
2470      assert(union_clan_ob.is_union() &amp;&amp; union_clan_ob.is_master_clan());
2471      map members = union_clan_ob.get_union_members().deep_dup();
2472      string union_rid = union_clan_ob.get_union_rid();
2473      string union_name = union_clan_ob.get_union_name();
2474      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : members)
2475      {
2476          if(clan_rid == union_clan_ob.get_rid())
2477              continue;
2478          int union_star = union_clan_ob.get_union_star();
2479          int clan_star = clan_info.clan_star ?? 0;
2480          mixed rpc_ret = rpc_notify_clan_union_dismiss(clan_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid, false);
2481          if(!is_array(rpc_ret) || !rpc_ret[0].ok)
2482          {
2483              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:union_formal_dismiss failed, 通知宗门解散失败，ret=%M, union_rid=%s, clan_info=%M", rpc_ret, union_rid, clan_info);
2484              continue;
2485          }
2486  
2487          map ret = remove_union_clan_member(union_rid, clan_rid, false);
2488          if(!ret.ok)
2489          {
2490              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:union_formal_dismiss failed, ret=%M, union_rid=%s, clan_info=%M", ret, union_rid, clan_info);
2491              continue;
2492          }
2493          catch(logex.union_gang_change_log("system", union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_clan_ob.get_union_name(), get_match_servers(), 
2494          clan_info.clan_rid, clan_info.clan_name, clan_star, 6, union_star, union_star - clan_star));
2495      }
2496  
2497      // 最后解散自己
2498      if(union_clan_ob.get_union_members().length() == 1)
2499      {
2500          map ret = remove_union_clan_member(union_clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_clan_ob.get_rid(), false);
2501          if(!ret.ok)
2502          {
2503              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:union_formal_dismiss 仙盟解散失败,union_rid=%s, union_name=%s, ret=%M", union_rid, union_name, ret); 
2504              return false;
2505          }
2506          TRACE_WARN("UnionD:union_formal_dismiss 仙盟解散成功,union_rid=%s, union_name=%s", union_rid, union_name); 
2507          string mail_id = get_config("disband_union_mail");
2508          // 邮件通知 仙盟创建
2509          co_pool_execute((array rids){
2510              for(string user_rid : rids)
2511                  MailD.send_content_mail(mail_id, user_rid);
2512          }, union_clan_ob.get_clan_members_keys());
2513          return true;
2514      }
2515      TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:union_formal_dismiss 仙盟解散失败,union_rid=%s, union_name=%s", union_rid, union_name); 
2516      return false; 
2517  }

2598      });

2601          return [false];
2602      }

2606          if(clan_info.clan_server_id == this_server_id)

2613          
2614      }
2615      return [true];

2663  public map master_check_union_valid(string union_rid, string clan_rid, string clan_server_id, map union_data)
2664  {

2666          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: master_check_union_valid :), unpack($&lt;));
2667      object union_clan_ob = get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_rid);
2668      if(!union_clan_ob)
2669      {
2670          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:master_check_union_valid union_clan_ob == nil, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s, clan_server_id=%s, union_data=%O", union_rid,clan_rid,clan_server_id,union_data);
2671          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_not_exist"};
2672      }
2673      map master_union_data = union_clan_ob.get_union_data();
2674      if(!master_union_data.members[clan_rid])
2675      {
2676          TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:master_check_union_valid not union member, union_rid=%s, clan_rid=%s, clan_server_id=%s, union_data=%O", union_rid,clan_rid,clan_server_id,union_data);
2677          return {"ok":false,"reason":"not_clan_members"};
2678      }
2679      return {"ok":true};
2680  }

2684  public void union_broadcast_self_text(string union_rid, string text_id, string send_rid, string content, map broadcast_info, array other_cmd)
2685  {
2686      map members = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
2687          object union_clan_ob = nil;
2688          for(string __clan_rid, object clan_ob : clan.get_clans())
2689          {
2690              if(!clan_ob.is_union())
2691                  continue;
2692              if(clan_ob.get_union_rid() == union_rid)
2693              {
2694                  union_clan_ob = clan_ob;
2695                  break;
2696              }
2697          }
2698  
2699          if(!union_clan_ob)
2700              return nil;
2701          return union_clan_ob.get_union_members();
2702      });
2703  
2704      for(string clan_rid, map clan_info : members)
2705      {
2706          rpc_union_broadcast_self_text(clan_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid, text_id, send_rid, content, broadcast_info, other_cmd);

2710  public void rpc_union_broadcast_self_text(string server_id, string clan_rid, string text_id, string send_rid, string content, map broadcast_info, array other_cmd)
2711  {
2712      RPC_POST_IN_SERVER;
2713      union_broadcast_self_text_imp(clan_rid, text_id, send_rid, content, broadcast_info, other_cmd);
2714  }

2716  void union_broadcast_self_text_imp(string clan_rid, string text_id, string send_rid, string content, map broadcast_info, array other_cmd)
2717  {
2718      object clan_obj = clan=&gt;get_clan(clan_rid);
2719      if(!clan_obj)
2720          return;
2721  
2722      for(string rid: clan_obj=&gt;get_clan_members_keys())
2723      {
2724          object user = UserD.get_user(rid);
2725          if(user)
2726          {
2727              user.send("broadcast", "broadcast_self", text_id, content, send_rid, broadcast_info);
2728              if (other_cmd != nil)
2729              {
2730                  user.send(unpack(other_cmd), content);
2731              }
2732          }
2733      }
2734  }

2745  public void rpc_notify_clan_enter_union(string server_id, string clan_rid)
2746  {

2749          on_clan_enter_union(clan_rid);
2750      });

2752  
2753  void on_clan_enter_union(string clan_rid)
2754  {
2755      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
2756          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: on_clan_enter_union :), unpack($&lt;));
2757      // 从所有仙盟对象的已邀请列表移除
2758      for(string __rid, object clan_ob : clan.get_clans())
2759      {
2760          if(!clan_ob.is_union())
2761              continue;
2762          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
2763          if(!union_data || !union_data.invite_clan_list)
2764              continue;
2765          if(!union_data.invite_clan_list[clan_rid])
2766              continue;
2767          union_data.invite_clan_list.delete(clan_rid);
2768          clan_ob.set_dirty(true);
2769      }
2770  }

2772  // region webgm查询接口
2773  
2774  public array gm_calc_clan_star_list(array server_ids, int top_count = 10)
2775  {
2776      map rank_infos = gen_match_range_battle_rank(server_ids);
2777      array clan_list = [];
2778      for(string server_id : server_ids)
2779      {
2780          mixed ret = rpc_gm_calc_clan_stars(server_id, rank_infos, top_count);
2781          if(!is_array(ret))
2782          {
2783              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:broadcast_match_range_battle_rank_info rpc_error server_id = %s, ret = %s", server_id, ret);
2784          }
2785          else if(ret.length() &gt; 0)
2786          {
2787              clan_list.push_back_n(ret);
2788          }
2789      }
2790      clan_list.sort((map a, map b)
2791      {
2792          return a.total_score &gt; b.total_score
2793      });
2794      return clan_list[0..top_count];
2795  }
2796  
2797  public array rpc_gm_calc_clan_stars(string server_id, map rank_data, int top_count)
2798  {
2799      RPC_GET_IN_SERVER;
2800      return gm_calc_clan_stars(rank_data, top_count);
2801  }
2802  
2803  array gm_calc_clan_stars(map rank_infos, int top_count)
2804  {
2805      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
2806          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: gm_calc_clan_stars :), unpack($&lt;));
2807      array clan_list = [];
2808      for(string __clan_rid, object clan_ob : clan.get_clans())
2809      {
2810          array user_score_list = [];
2811          map rank_list = rank_infos.player_dict;
2812          int min_combat_capacity = rank_infos.min_combat_capacity;
2813          int total_score = 0;
2814          for(string __user_rid, map member : clan_ob.get_members())
2815          {
2816              int rank = get_last_range_rank();
2817              int log_rank = 0;
2818              if(rank_list[member.rid])
2819              {
2820                  rank = rank_list[member.rid].rank;
2821                  log_rank = rank;
2822              } 
2823              else if(member.combat_capacity &gt; min_combat_capacity)
2824              {
2825                  // 玩家不在排行榜内，但是玩家战力已入榜
2826                  for(string __rid, map player_info : rank_list)
2827                  {
2828                      if(member.combat_capacity &gt; player_info.combat_capacity &amp;&amp; player_info.rank &lt; rank)
2829                      {
2830                          rank = player_info.rank;
2831                          log_rank = rank;
2832                      }
2833                  }
2834                  rank = math.max(1, rank - 1);
2835              }
2836              int score = get_score_by_user_battle_rank(rank);
2837              total_score += score;
2838              user_score_list.push_back(log_rank);
2839          }
2840          int star = get_clan_star_by_score(total_score);
2841          user_score_list.sort();
2842  
2843          map clan_data = {};
2844          clan_data.clan_rid = clan_ob.get_rid();
2845          clan_data.clan_name = clan_ob.get_name();
2846          clan_data.server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
2847          clan_data.star = star;
2848          clan_data.total_score = total_score;
2849          clan_data.rank_list = user_score_list;
2850          clan_data.member_count = clan_ob.get_member_num();
2851          clan_data.owner = clan_ob.get_owner();
2852          clan_data.owner_name = clan_ob.get_owner_name();
2853          clan_list.push_back(clan_data);
2854      }
2855  
2856      clan_list.sort((map a, map b) 
2857      {
2858          return a.total_score &gt; b.total_score
2859      });
2860  
2861      return clan_list[0..top_count - 1];
2862  }
2863  // endregion

 27  // 玩家请求创建仙盟
 28  public map request_create_union(object user, map context)
 29  {
 30      map ret = check_can_create_union(user, context);
 31      if(!ret.ok)
 32          return ret;
 33      // 扣除物资
 34      let int item_id, int item_amount = UnionD.get_config("union_create_cost");
 35      assert(item_id == CLAN_SUPPLIES);
 36      if(!user.cost_clan_supplies(item_amount, "create_union"))
 37      {
 38          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_cost_error"};
 39      }
 40  
 41      map clan_info = UnionD.gen_union_clan_info(ret.clan_ob);
 42      assert(clan_info != nil);
 43      UnionD.create_union(user.get_rid(), user.get_name(), context.name, clan_info, ret.clan_ob);
 44      return {"ok":true,"reason":nil};
 45  }
 46  
 47  map check_can_create_union(object user, map context)
 48  {
 49      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
 50      array name_ret = UnionD.is_valid_union_name(user, context.name);
 51      if(!name_ret[0])
 52      {
 53          if(name_ret[1])
 54              user.show_common_tip(name_ret[1]);
 55          return {"ok":false};
 56      }
 57  
 58      if(!UnionD.is_union_open())
 59      {
 60          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
 61      }
 62  
 63      let bool check_ret, string check_reason = UnionD.can_create_union(user.get_clan_rid());
 64  
 65      if(!check_ret)
 66      {
 67          string reason = check_reason ?? "union_op_ban";
 68          return {"ok":false,"reason": reason};
 69      }
 70  
 71      // 玩家是否是宗主
 72      return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
 73          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
 74          if(!clan_ob)
 75          {
 76              return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_no_clan"};
 77          }
 78          if(clan_ob.get_owner() !=  user_rid)
 79          {
 80              return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_no_power"};
 81          }
 82  
 83          if(clan_ob.is_union())
 84          {
 85              return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_error_state"};
 86          }
 87  
 88          int clan_star = clan_ob.get_star();
 89  
 90          if(clan_star &lt; UnionD.get_config("union_basic_gang_star"))
 91          {
 92              return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_star_error"};
 93          }
 94          return {"ok":true, "clan_ob" : clan_ob};
 95      });
 96  }

100  {

108  
109  void on_user_get_union_list(object user, string search_name)
110  {
111      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
112          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: on_user_get_union_list :), unpack($&lt;));
113      defer {user=&gt;delete_temp("is_request_union_list")};
114      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user.get_rid());
115      if(!clan_ob)
116          return;
117      int clan_star = clan_ob.get_star();
118      map apply_union_list = clan_ob.query("apply_union_list") ?? {};
119      map reject_apply_union_list = clan_ob.query("reject_apply_union_list") ?? {};
120      map union_list = UnionD.get_union_list_from_match_range();
121      int cur_time = time.time();
122      int reject_timeout = UnionD.get_config("union_reject_apply_cooldown") ?? 1;
123      reject_timeout = reject_timeout * 60;
124      int union_max_star = UnionD.get_config("union_max_star");
125      array ret = [];
126      // 刷新状态
127      for(string union_rid, map union_info : union_list)
128      {
129          if(!is_empty(search_name) &amp;&amp; strsrch(union_info.union_name, search_name) == -1)
130              continue;
131          ret.push_back(union_info);
132          if(reject_apply_union_list[union_rid])
133          {
134              int reject_time = reject_apply_union_list[union_rid];
135              if(cur_time - reject_time &lt; reject_timeout)
136              {
137                  union_info.apply_status = UNION_APPLY_STATUS.REJECTED;
138                  union_info.reject_time = reject_time;
139                  continue;
140              }
141              else
142              {
143                  reject_apply_union_list.delete(union_rid);
144              }
145          }
146          if(apply_union_list[union_rid])
147          {
148              union_info.apply_status = UNION_APPLY_STATUS.APPLIED;
149              continue;
150          }
151  
152          if(clan_star + union_info.union_star &lt;= union_max_star)
153          {
154              union_info.apply_status = UNION_APPLY_STATUS.CAN_APPLY;
155          }
156          else
157          {
158              union_info.apply_status = UNION_APPLY_STATUS.OVER_LIMIT;
159          }
160      }
161      user.send("union_op", "list", ret, UnionD.get_match_servers());
162  }

164  // 申请加入某个仙盟
165  public map request_apply_union(object user, string union_rid, string server_id)
166  {
167      map check_ret = check_can_apply(user, union_rid, server_id);
168      if(!check_ret.ok)
169      {
170          return check_ret;
171      }
172  
173      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(union_rid, server_id))
174      {
175          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
176      }
177  
178      // 发给目标仙盟
179      map apply_info = check_ret.apply_info;
180      string clan_rid = user.get_clan_rid();
181      string clan_name = user.get_clan_name();
182      map apply_ret = UnionD.apply_union(union_rid, server_id, apply_info);
183      if(apply_ret.ok)
184      {
185          // 申请成功之后，加入到已申请列表
186          function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
187              object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
188              if(!clan_ob)
189                  return;
190              map apply_union_list = clan_ob.query("apply_union_list") ?? {};
191              apply_union_list[union_rid] = true;
192              clan_ob.set("apply_union_list", apply_union_list);
193              clan_ob.set_dirty(true);
194          });
195      }
196      else
197          return apply_ret;
198      catch(logex.union_apply_log(user.get_rid(), clan_rid, clan_name, UnionD.get_match_servers(), apply_ret.clan_star ?? 0, union_rid, apply_ret.union_name, apply_ret.union_star ?? 0, 1));
199      return {"ok":true, "reason" : nil};
200  }
201  
202  map check_can_apply(object user, string union_rid, string server_id)
203  {
204      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
205      {
206          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
207      }
208  
209      // 处理结盟邀请和申请结盟权限用同一条
210      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
211      if(!clan_data)
212      {
213          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
214      }
215      // 是否有权限
216      if(!clan.check_power(clan_data.power, "union_request"))
217      {
218          return {"ok":false, "reason" : TT("unionuserd_51322")};
219      }
220  
221      if(clan_data.union_rid)
222      {
223          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_state"};
224      }
225      string clan_rid = clan_data.rid;
226      
227      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
228          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
229          if(!clan_ob)
230          {
231              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
232          }
233          int cur_time = time.time();
234          // 判定是否已被拒绝
235          int reject_timeout = UnionD.get_config("union_reject_apply_cooldown") ?? 1;
236          reject_timeout = reject_timeout * 60;
237          map reject_apply_union_list = clan_ob.query("reject_apply_union_list") ?? {};
238          if(reject_apply_union_list[union_rid])
239          {
240              int reject_time = reject_apply_union_list[union_rid];
241              if(cur_time - reject_time &lt; reject_timeout)
242              {
243                  return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_cold"};
244              }
245              else
246              {
247                  reject_apply_union_list.delete(union_rid);
248              }
249          }
250          map clan_info = gen_clan_apply_info(clan_ob);
251          return {"ok":true, "reason" : nil,"apply_info":clan_info};
252      });
253  
254      return ret;
255  }
256  
257  map gen_clan_apply_info(object clan_ob)
258  {
259      map clan_info = {};
260      clan_info.clan_rid = clan_ob.get_rid();
261      clan_info.clan_server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
262      clan_info.clan_name = clan_ob.get_name();
263      clan_info.level = clan_ob.query("level");
264      clan_info.star = clan_ob.get_star();
265      clan_info.owner = clan_ob.get_owner_name();
266      clan_info.member_count = clan_ob.get_member_num();
267      clan_info.combat_capacity = clan_ob.query("combat_capacity") ?? 0;
268      return clan_info;
269  }
270  
271  // 盟主或副盟主审批申请
272  public map request_confirm_apply(object user, string target_clan_rid, bool agree = true)
273  {
274      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
275      {
276          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
277      }
278      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
279      if(!clan_data || !clan_data.union_rid)
280      {
281          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_clan"};
282      }
283      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
284      string union_rid = clan_data.union_rid;
285      string union_server_id = clan_data.union_server_id;
286      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "request"))
287      {
288          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
289      }
290  
291      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(union_rid, union_server_id))
292      {
293          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
294      }
295  
296      string clan_rid = clan_data.rid;
297      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
298          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
299          if(!clan_ob)
300          {
301              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
302          }
303  
304          //if(!clan_ob.is_union() || !clan_ob.is_master_clan())
305          if(!clan_ob.is_union())
306          {
307              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_state"};
308          }
309  
310          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
311          map clan_info = nil;
312          map union_apply_list = nil;
313          if(union_data &amp;&amp; union_data.union_apply_list &amp;&amp; union_data.union_apply_list[target_clan_rid])
314          {
315              union_apply_list = union_data.union_apply_list;
316              clan_info = union_apply_list[target_clan_rid];
317              union_apply_list.delete(target_clan_rid);
318              clan_ob.set_union_data_filed("union_apply_list", union_apply_list, true, true);
319          }
320          else
321          {
322              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_apply_not_exist"};
323          }
324  
325          int union_star = clan_ob.get_union_star();
326          if(!agree)
327          {
328              // 通知申请被拒绝了
329              UnionD.rpc_notify_apply_reject(clan_info.clan_server_id, clan_info.clan_rid, union_rid);
330  
331              logex.union_apply_log("system", clan_info.clan_rid, clan_info.clan_name, UnionD.get_match_servers(), clan_info.star ?? 0,
332              clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_ob.get_union_name(), union_star, 3);
333  
334              return {"ok":true};
335          }
336          else
337          {
338              map ret = UnionD.clan_join_union(clan_ob, clan_info, 1);
339              int apply_log_type = 4;
340              if(ret.ok)
341                  apply_log_type = 2;
342              logex.union_apply_log("system", clan_info.clan_rid, clan_info.clan_name, UnionD.get_match_servers(), clan_info.star ?? 0,
343              clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_ob.get_union_name(), union_star, apply_log_type);
344              return ret;
345          }
346      });
347      return ret;
348  }
349  
350  
351  // 盟主或副盟主查询未加入仙盟的宗门列表
352  public void request_un_join_clan_list(object user, string search_name)
353  {
354      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
355      if(!clan_data)
356      {
357          return;
358      }
359      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
360      {
361          return;
362      }
363      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
364      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "request"))
365      {
366          return;
367      }
368  
369      if(user.query_temp("is_request_un_join_clan_list"))
370          return;
371      user.set_temp("is_request_un_join_clan_list", true);
372      string clan_rid = clan_data.rid;
373      let map invite_clan_list, map reject_clan_list = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
374          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
375          if(!clan_ob)
376              return[{}, {}];
377          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
378          return [union_data.invite_clan_list ?? {}, union_data.reject_clan_list ?? {}];
379      });
380  
381      co_pool_execute((){
382          on_user_get_un_join_clan_list(user, invite_clan_list, reject_clan_list, search_name);
383      });
384  }
385  
386  void on_user_get_un_join_clan_list(object user, map invite_clan_list, map reject_clan_list, string search_name)
387  {
388      defer{user=&gt;delete_temp("is_request_un_join_clan_list")};
389      array clan_list = UnionD.get_un_union_clan_list_in_range(UnionD.get_match_servers());
390      int reject_timeout = UnionD.get_config("union_reject_apply_cooldown") ?? 1;
391      reject_timeout = reject_timeout * 60;
392      int cur_time = time.time();
393      // 循环遍历，计算宗门状态
394      array ret = [];
395      for(map info : clan_list)
396      {
397          if(!is_empty(search_name) &amp;&amp; strsrch(info.clan_name, search_name) == -1)
398              continue;
399          ret.push_back(info);
400          int state = (int)UNION_INVITE_STATUS.CAN_INVITE;
401          if(invite_clan_list[info.clan_rid])
402          {
403              state = (int)UNION_INVITE_STATUS.INVITED;
404          }
405          else if(reject_clan_list[info.clan_rid])
406          {
407              int reject_time = reject_clan_list[info.clan_rid];
408              if(cur_time - reject_time &gt; reject_timeout)
409              {
410                  state = (int)UNION_INVITE_STATUS.CAN_INVITE;
411              }
412              else
413              {
414                  state = (int)UNION_INVITE_STATUS.REJECTED;
415                  info.reject_time = reject_time;
416              }
417          }
418          info.invite_status = state;
419      }
420  
421      user.send("union_op", "clan_list", ret);
422  }
