598  // 禅让仙盟盟主
599  public map request_appoint_union_owner(object user, string clan_rid, string owner_rid)
600  {
601      map ret = check_can_appoint_union_owner(user, clan_rid, owner_rid);
602      if(!ret.ok)
603          return ret;
604      map clan_info = ret.clan_info;
605      string clan_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(clan_info.clan_server_id);
606      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
607      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_data.union_rid, SystemD.get_server_id()))
608      {
609          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
610      }
611      return UnionD.appoint_union_owner(ret.union_clan_ob, clan_server_id, clan_rid, owner_rid);
612  }
613  
614  map check_can_appoint_union_owner(object user, string clan_rid, string owner_rid)
615  {
616      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
617      {
618          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
619      }
620      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
621      if(!clan_data)
622          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_clan"};
623      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
624          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
625      if(clan_data.union_power != (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER)
626      {
627          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
628      }
629      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
630      return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(),(){
631          object union_clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
632          if(!union_clan_ob)
633              return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_clan"};
634          if(!union_clan_ob.is_master_clan())
635              return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
636          map members = union_clan_ob.get_union_members();
637          map clan_info = members[clan_rid];
638          if(!clan_info)
639              return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_error_param"};
640          return {"ok":true, "clan_info":clan_info, "union_clan_ob":union_clan_ob};
641      });
642  }

644  // 宗主请求退出当前仙盟
645  public map request_exit_union_clan(object user)
646  {
647      map ret = check_can_exit_union_clan(user);
648      if(!ret.ok)
649          return ret;
650      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
651      string union_server_id = clan_data.union_server_id;
652      string clan_rid = clan_data.rid;
653      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_data.union_rid, union_server_id))
654      {
655          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
656      }
657      let array rids, int member_count, string union_rid,string union_name, bool is_master_clan, object clan_ob, int union_star = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
658          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
659          if(!clan_ob)
660              return [nil, false, nil];
661          string union_name = clan_ob.get_union_name();
662          return [clan_ob.get_clan_members_keys(), clan_ob.get_union_members().length(), clan_ob.get_union_rid(), union_name, clan_ob.is_master_clan(), clan_ob, clan_ob.get_union_star()];
663      });
664      union_server_id = remote.get_merged_server_id(union_server_id);
665      mixed rpc_ret = UnionD.rpc_remove_union_clan_member(union_server_id, union_rid, clan_rid);
666      if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
667      {
668          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_rpc_error"}
669      }
670      ret = rpc_ret[0];
671      if(ret.ok)
672      {
673          // 不是主宗门，需要调用union_dismiss
674          if(!is_master_clan)
675          {
676              function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
677                  object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
678                  if(!clan_ob)
679                      return;
680                  clan_ob.union_dismiss(false, union_rid);
681              });
682          }
683  
684  
685          if(rids)
686          {
687              string mail_id = member_count == 1 ? UnionD.get_config("disband_union_mail") : UnionD.get_config("exit_gang_mail");
688              co_pool_execute((array rids) {
689                  for(string user_rid : rids)
690                  {
691                      if(member_count == 1)
692                          MailD.send_content_mail(mail_id, user_rid);
693                      else
694                          MailD.send_content_mail(mail_id, user_rid, [union_name]);
695                  }
696              }, rids)
697          }
698          int clan_star = clan_ob=&gt;get_star();
699          logex.union_gang_change_log("system", union_rid, union_name, UnionD.get_match_servers(), clan_rid, clan_ob.get_name(), 
700          clan_star, 3, union_star, union_star - clan_star);
701      }
702  
703      return ret;
704  }

711      }
712      map clan_data = user.query("clan");

730  // 修改仙盟名称
731  public map request_set_union_name(object user, string name)
732  {
733      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
734      {
735          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
736      }
737      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
738      if(!clan_data)
739      {
740          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
741      }
742      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
743      {
744          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
745      }
746      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
747      string old_union_name = clan_data.union_name;
748      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "rename"))
749      {
750          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
751      }
752      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
753      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
754          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
755          if(!clan_ob)
756              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
757          if(!clan_ob.is_union())
758              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_union"};
759          if(clan_ob.get_union_name() == name)
760              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_param"};
761          int cold_time = UnionD.get_config("union_change_name_cooldown") * HOUR_TIME;
762          map union_dbase = clan_ob.get_union_data();
763          int last_change_name_time = union_dbase.union_last_change_name_time ?? 0;
764          if(time.time() - last_change_name_time &lt; cold_time)
765              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_cold"};
766          return {"ok":true, "union_dbase" : union_dbase};
767      });
768  
769      if(!ret.ok)
770          return ret;
771  
772      // 判定名称是否合法
773      array name_ret = UnionD.is_valid_union_name(user, name);
774      if(!name_ret[0])
775      {
776          user.show_common_tip(name_ret[1]);
777          return {"ok":false};
778      }
779  
780      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_data.union_rid, clan_data.union_server_id))
781      {
782          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
783      }
784  
785      // 扣除物资
786      let int item_id, int item_amount = UnionD.get_config("union_change_name_cost");
787      assert(item_id == CLAN_SUPPLIES);
788      if(!user.cost_clan_supplies(item_amount, "union_change_name"))
789      {
790          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_cost_error"};
791      }
792  
793      // rpc 通知主宗门进行改名
794      map union_dbase = ret.union_dbase;
795      mixed rpc_ret = UnionD.rpc_union_change_name(union_dbase.union_server_id, union_dbase.union_rid, name);
796      if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
797      {
798          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_rpc_error"};
799      }
800      if(!rpc_ret[0].ok)
801      {
802          // 修改失败，返回玩家的物资消耗
803          function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
804              object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
805              if(!clan_ob)
806                  return;
807              clan_ob.add_clan_supplies(item_amount, "union_rename_return");
808              TRACE_WARN("UnionUserD:rpc_union_change_name failed, 返还宗门物资,item_amount=%d, user=%s", item_amount, user.desc());
809          });
810          
811          return rpc_ret[0];
812      }
813  
814      logex.union_name_change_log(user_rid, clan_data.union_rid, UnionD.get_match_servers(), old_union_name, name, user_rid, user.get_name(), union_power);
815          
816      return {"ok":true};
817  }

819  // 修改仙盟公告
820  public map request_set_union_info(object user, string info, bool broadcast)
821  {
822      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
823      {
824          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
825      }
826      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
827      if(!clan_data)
828      {
829          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
830      }
831      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
832      {
833          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
834      }
835      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
836      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "info"))
837      {
838          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
839      }
840  
841      // 判定名称是否合法
842      array name_ret = UnionD.is_valid_union_info(user, info);
843      if(!name_ret[0])
844      {
845          if(name_ret[1])
846              user.show_common_tip(name_ret[1]);
847          return {"ok":false};
848      }
849      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
850      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (bool broadcast){
851          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
852          if(!clan_ob)
853              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
854          if(!clan_ob.is_union())
855              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_union"};
856          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
857          if(union_data.info == info)
858              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_param"};
859          if(broadcast)
860          {
861              int cold_time = UnionD.get_config("union_announce_mail_cooldown");
862              map union_dbase = clan_ob.get_union_data();
863              int last_change_info_time = union_dbase.union_last_change_info_mail_time ?? 0;
864              if(time.time() - last_change_info_time &lt; cold_time)
865                  broadcast = false;
866              else
867              {
868                  // 判定每日次数
869                  int union_change_info_times = union_dbase.union_change_info_times ?? 0;
870                  if(union_change_info_times &gt;=  UnionD.get_config("union_announce_mail_max_time"))
871                      broadcast = false;
872              }
873          }
874          return {"ok":true,"union_dbase" : clan_ob.get_union_data(), "broadcast" : broadcast};
875      }, broadcast);
876  
877      if(!ret.ok)
878          return ret;
879  
880      if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_data.union_rid, clan_data.union_server_id))
881      {
882          return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
883      }
884  
885      broadcast = ret.broadcast;
886      // rpc 通知主宗门进行改公告
887      map union_dbase = ret.union_dbase;
888      mixed rpc_ret = UnionD.rpc_union_change_info(union_dbase.union_server_id, union_dbase.union_rid, info, broadcast, user.get_rid());
889      if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
890      {
891          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_rpc_error"};
892      }
893      return rpc_ret[0];
894  }

 943  
 944  map can_tick_clan_member(object user, string clan_rid)
 945  {
 946      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
 947      {
 948          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
 949      }
 950      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
 951      if(!clan_data)
 952      {
 953          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
 954      }
 955      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
 956      {
 957          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
 958      }
 959      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
 960      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "kick_gang"))
 961      {
 962          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
 963      }
 964      return {"ok":true};
 965  }
 966  
 967  // 仙盟盟主任免副盟主
 968  public map request_appoint_union_vice_owner(object user, string clan_rid, string target_user_rid)
 969  {
 970      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
 971      {
 972          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
 973      }
 974      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
 975      if(!clan_data)
 976      {
 977          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
 978      }
 979      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
 980      {
 981          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
 982      }
 983      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
 984      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "appoint_power_2"))
 985      {
 986          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
 987      }
 988  
 989      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
 990      return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
 991          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
 992          if(!clan_ob)
 993              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
 994          if(!clan_ob.is_union() || !clan_ob.is_master_clan())
 995              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_union"};
 996          map union_members = clan_ob.get_union_members();
 997          map clan_member_info = union_members[clan_rid];
 998          if(!clan_member_info)
 999              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_param"};
1000          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
1001          int cur_count = union_data.union_vice_owner_num;
1002          if(cur_count &gt;= UnionD.get_config("max_vice_leader"))
1003          {
1004              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_reach_max_count"};
1005          }
1006          if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_ob.get_union_server_id()))
1007          {
1008              return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
1009          }
1010          mixed rpc_ret = UnionD.rpc_set_union_vice_owner(clan_member_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid, target_user_rid);
1011          if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
1012          {
1013              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:rpc_set_union_vice_owner error, ret=%M, clan_member_info=%M, target_user_rid=%s, user=%s", rpc_ret, clan_member_info, target_user_rid, user.desc());
1014              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_rpc_error"};
1015          }
1016  
1017          if(!rpc_ret[0].ok)
1018          {
1019              return rpc_ret[0];
1020          }
1021  
1022          // 修改副宗主数量，并同步
1023          clan_ob.set_vice_owner_count(cur_count + 1);
1024          return {"ok":true};
1025      });
1026  }
1027  
1028  // 仙盟盟主罢免副盟主
1029  public map request_remove_union_vice_owner(object user, string clan_rid, string target_user_rid)
1030  {
1031      if(UnionD.is_union_op_ban())
1032      {
1033          return {"ok":false,"reason":"union_op_ban"};
1034      }
1035      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
1036      if(!clan_data)
1037      {
1038          return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
1039      }
1040      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
1041      {
1042          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_union"};
1043      }
1044      int union_power = clan_data.union_power;
1045      if(!UnionD.check_power(union_power, "remove_power_2"))
1046      {
1047          return {"ok":false, "reason":"union_no_power"};
1048      }
1049  
1050      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
1051      return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (){
1052          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user_rid);
1053          if(!clan_ob)
1054              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_clan"};
1055          if(!clan_ob.is_union() || !clan_ob.is_master_clan())
1056              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_no_union"};
1057          map union_members = clan_ob.get_union_members();
1058          map clan_member_info = union_members[clan_rid];
1059          if(!clan_member_info)
1060              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_error_param"};
1061          map union_data = clan_ob.get_union_data();
1062          int cur_count = union_data.union_vice_owner_num;
1063          if(!UnionD.check_union_rpc_alive(clan_ob.get_union_rid(), clan_ob.get_union_server_id()))
1064          {
1065              return {"ok":false, "reason":"I18n_ID_1195"};
1066          }
1067          mixed rpc_ret = UnionD.rpc_cancel_union_vice_owner(clan_member_info.clan_server_id, clan_rid, target_user_rid);
1068          if(!is_array(rpc_ret))
1069          {
1070              TRACE_ERROR("UnionD:rpc_cancel_union_vice_owner error, ret=%M, clan_member_info=%M, target_user_rid=%s, user=%s", rpc_ret, clan_member_info, target_user_rid, user.desc());
1071              return {"ok":false, "reason" : "union_rpc_error"};
1072          }
1073  
1074          if(!rpc_ret[0].ok)
1075          {
1076              return rpc_ret[0];
1077          }
1078  
1079          // 修改副宗主数量，并同步
1080          clan_ob.set_vice_owner_count(math.max(0, cur_count - 1));
1081          return {"ok":true};
1082      });
1083  }
1084  
1085  // 查看仙盟详情
1086  public map request_union_info(object user, string server_id, string union_rid)
1087  {
1088      mixed ret = UnionD.rpc_get_union_info(server_id, union_rid);
1089      if(!is_array(ret))
1090          return nil;
1091      return ret[0];
1092  }</message>

102  
103  // 宗门的星级
104  public int get_star()
105  {
106      return this.query("star") ?? 0;
107  }
108  
109  // 是否是主宗门
110  public bool is_master_clan()
111  {
112      map union_data = get_union_data();
113      if(!union_data)
114          return false;
115      return union_data.union_master_clan_rid == this.get_rid();
116  }
117  
118  public string get_union_master_clan_rid()
119  {
120      map union_data = get_union_data();
121      if(!union_data)
122          return nil;
123      return union_data.union_master_clan_rid;
124  }
125  
126  public map get_union_data()
127  {
128      return this.query("union_dbase");
129  }
130  
131  // 创建新的仙盟
132  public void new_union_data(map union_dbase)
133  {
134      assert(is_union() == false);
135      assert(union_dbase.members[this.get_rid()]);
136      this.set("union_dbase", union_dbase);
137      init_data(union_dbase);
138      refresh_union_fields();
139      this.broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "union_dbase", union_dbase);
140      if(is_master_clan())
141      {
142          // 创建仙盟
143          hook.invoke("UNION_CREATE", this);
144          // 仙盟增加成员
145          hook.invoke("UNION_ADD_CLAN_MEMBER", this, get_union_rid(), this.get_rid(), true);
146  
147          catch(upload_union_gang_right_log());
148      }
149      else
150      {
151          // 宗主默认设置为副盟主
152          string clan_owner = this.get_owner();
153          map member_data = this.get_member_data(clan_owner);
154          if(member_data)
155              member_data.union_power = (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER;
156          // 宗门加入仙盟
157          hook.invoke("UNION_JOIN", this);
158          catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", get_union_rid(), get_union_name(), gen_clan_member_log_desc(),
159          clan_owner, this.get_rid(), 0, (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER, 2));
160      }
161  
162      this.broadcast_atrrib_changed();
163      // 清空邀请信息
164      if(this.query("union_invite_list"))
165      {
166          this.delete("union_invite_list");
167          this.broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "union_invite_list",nil);
168      }
169      // 清空申请列表
170      if(this.query("apply_union_list"))
171      {
172          this.delete("apply_union_list");
173      }
174  }
175  
176  // 替换仙盟数据
177  public void replace_union_data(map union_dbase)
178  {
179      assert(is_union());
180      assert(union_dbase.members[this.get_rid()]);
181      bool is_master_clan_old = is_master_clan();
182      this.set("union_dbase", union_dbase);
183      init_data(union_dbase);
184      refresh_union_fields();
185      this.broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "union_dbase", union_dbase);
186      // 变成主宗门了
187      if(!is_master_clan_old &amp;&amp; is_master_clan())
188      {
189          string new_owner = union_dbase.union_owner;
190          map members = this.get_members();
191          map member_data = members[new_owner];
192          if(!member_data)
193          {
194              TRACE_ERROR("FUnion:replace_union_data owner_member_data == nil, union_dbase=%M, clan_ob=%s", union_dbase, this.desc());
195          }
196          else
197          {
198              if(member_data.union_power != (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER)
199              {
200                  // 设置为盟主
201                  this.refresh_member_data(new_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER});
202                  catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", get_union_rid(), get_union_name(), gen_clan_member_log_desc(),
203                  new_owner, this.get_rid(), (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER,(int)UNION_POWER.OWNER, 1));
204                  // 有主宗门退出了，刷新一下副盟主的数量
205                  co_pool_execute((){
206                      UnionD.refresh_union_vice_owner_num(this);
207                  });
208              }
209          }
210          UnionD.register_union_name(union_dbase.union_name, SystemD.get_server_id());
211  
212          catch(upload_union_gang_right_log(true));
213      }
214      this.save();
215      this.broadcast_atrrib_changed();
216      hook.invoke("UNION_REPLACE_DBASE", this);
217  }

275  public void refresh_union_fields()
276  {
277      map union_dbase = get_union_data();
278      int total_cc = 0;
279      int total_star = 0;
280      int total_member_count = 0;
281      for(string __clan_rid, map clan_info : union_dbase.members)
282      {
283          total_cc += clan_info.combat_capacity;
284          total_star += clan_info.clan_star;
285          total_member_count += clan_info.member_count;
286      }
287      union_dbase.union_combat_capacity = total_cc;
288      union_dbase.union_star = math.min(total_star, UnionD.get_config("union_max_star"));
289      union_dbase.union_member_count = total_member_count;
290  }

292  // 解散单个仙盟成员
293  public void union_dismiss(bool last_clan = false, string this_union_rid = nil)
294  {
295      string master_union_name = get_union_name();
296      string union_rid = _union_rid;
297      if(!union_rid)
298          union_rid = this_union_rid;
299      _union_rid := nil;
300      _union_server_id := nil;
301      _union_name := nil;
302      map union_members = get_union_members();
303      this.set("union_dbase", nil);
304      this.broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "union_dbase", nil);
305      // 清除所有成员的仙盟职位字段
306      for(string member_rid, map member_info : this.get_members())
307      {
308          int union_power = member_info.union_power ?? (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER;
309          if(union_power != (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER)
310          {
311              catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", union_rid, master_union_name, "",
312              member_rid, this.get_rid(), union_power, 0, 2));
313          }
314  
315          member_info.delete("union_power");
316      }
317      UnionD.board_clan_members_refresh_clan_data(this);
318      if(last_clan)
319      {
320          if(master_union_name)
321              UnionD.unregister_union_name(master_union_name, SystemD.get_server_id());
322          map clan_info = union_members[this.get_rid()];
323          // 仙盟移除成员
324          hook.invoke("UNION_REMOVE_CLAN_MEMBER", this, union_rid, this.get_rid(), nil, clan_info);
325          // 仙盟解散(仙盟最后剩下最后一个成员的时候调用)
326          hook.invoke("UNION_DISMISS", this, union_rid);
327      }
328      // 仙盟成员解散
329      hook.invoke("UNION_MEMBER_DISMISS", this, union_rid, last_clan);
330      this.broadcast_atrrib_changed();
331      this.delete("upload_clan_heritage_level");
332      this.save();
333  }

356  // 新增加入仙盟的请求
357  public map add_union_apply(map clan_info)
358  {
359      assert(is_union() &amp;&amp; is_master_clan());
360  
361      // 判定星际
362      if(clan_info.star + get_union_star() &gt; UnionD.get_config("union_max_star"))
363      {
364          return {"ok":false,"reason":TT("funion_17765")};
365      }
366      map union_data = get_union_data();
367      if(!union_data.union_apply_list)
368          union_data.union_apply_list = {};
369      map union_apply_list = union_data.union_apply_list;
370      if(!union_apply_list[clan_info.clan_rid])
371      {
372          union_apply_list[clan_info.clan_rid] = clan_info;
373          set_union_data_filed("union_apply_list", union_apply_list, true, true);
374      }
375      return {"ok":true, "union_name": get_union_name(), "union_star":get_union_star(), "clan_star":clan_info.star};
376  }

391  // 刷新宗门活跃标签
392  public void refresh_clan_union_active_flag()
393  {
394      bool active_flag = false;
395      int cur_time = time.time();
396      // 宗门成员所有成员2周未登录就算宗门不活跃了
397      int inactive_day = UnionD.get_config("clan_member_inactive_day") ?? 14;
398      int inactive_sec = inactive_day * DAY_TIME;
399      for(string __member_rid, map member_info : this.get_members())
400      {
401          if(is_int(member_info.state))
402          {
403              if(cur_time - member_info.state &lt; inactive_sec)
404              {
405                  active_flag = true;
406                  break;
407              }
408          }
409          else
410          {
411              active_flag = true;
412              break;
413          }
414      }
415      this.set_temp("union_active_flag", active_flag);
416  }

489  // 计算宗门星级
490  public void calc_clan_star(map rank_infos)
491  {
492      // 未开放
493      if(!UnionD.is_union_open())
494      {
495          TRACE_ERROR("FUnion:calc_clan_star error,union not open");
496          return;
497      }
498      array user_score_list = [];
499      map rank_list = rank_infos.player_dict;
500      int min_combat_capacity = rank_infos.min_combat_capacity;
501      int total_score = 0;
502      map score_detail = {};
503      for(string user_rid, map member : this.get_members())
504      {
505          int rank = UnionD.get_last_range_rank();
506          int log_rank = 0;
507          if(rank_list[member.rid])
508          {
509              rank = rank_list[member.rid].rank;
510              log_rank = rank;
511          } 
512          else if(member.combat_capacity &gt; min_combat_capacity)
513          {
514              // 玩家不在排行榜内，但是玩家战力已入榜
515              for(string __rid, map player_info : rank_list)
516              {
517                  if(member.combat_capacity &gt; player_info.combat_capacity &amp;&amp; player_info.rank &lt; rank)
518                  {
519                      rank = player_info.rank;
520                      log_rank = rank;
521                  }
522              }
523              rank = math.max(1, rank - 1);
524              TRACE_WARN("FUnion:calc_clan_star 玩家未入榜，但是战力超过最低值，重新计算玩家排行,rank=%d, combat_capacity=%M, user_rid=%M", rank, member.combat_capacity, member.rid);
525          }
526          int score = UnionD.get_score_by_user_battle_rank(rank);
527          total_score += score;
528          score_detail[user_rid] = {"rank":rank, "score":score};
529          // 保存一份玩家积分信息，方便后续查证
530          member.score_detail = [rank, score];
531          user_score_list.push_back(log_rank);
532      }
533      this.set_temp("member_score_detail", score_detail);
534      int star = UnionD.get_clan_star_by_score(total_score);
535      int old_star = get_star();
536      TRACE_WARN("FUnion:calc_clan_star 设置宗门星级total_score = %d, old_star = %d, star=%d, clan_ob=%s", total_score, old_star, star, this.desc());
537      user_score_list.sort();
538      logex.gang_star_log("system", this.get_rid(), this.get_name(), UnionD.get_match_servers(), SystemD.get_server_id(), star, user_score_list);
539      if(old_star == star)
540      {
541          this.save();
542          return;
543      }
544      this.set_and_broadcast("star", star);
545      if(is_union())
546      {
547          // 同步给主仙盟，主仙盟再同步给成员
548          string union_server_id = get_union_server_id();
549          string union_rid = get_union_rid();
550          map data = {};
551          data.clan_star = star;
552          data.combat_capacity = this.query("combat_capacity");
553          data.member_count = this.get_member_num();
554          UnionD.sync_clan_member_data(union_server_id, union_rid, this.get_rid(), data);
555      }
556      this.save();
557  }

559  // 收到同步的宗门成员数据
560  public void on_sync_union_member_data(string clan_rid, map data)
561  {
562      if(!is_union())
563      {
564          TRACE_ERROR("FUnion:on_sync_union_member_data not union, clan_rid=%s, data=%M", clan_rid, data);
565          return;
566      }
567      map members = get_union_members();
568      map clan_member_info = members[clan_rid];
569      // 成员不存在
570      if(!clan_member_info)
571      {
572          TRACE_ERROR("FUnion:on_sync_union_member_data clan_member_info == nil, clan_rid=%s, data=%M", clan_rid, data);
573          return;
574      }
575  
576      bool refresh_clan_attrib = false;
577      if(data.clan_heritage_attribs &amp;&amp; !is_equal_value(data.clan_heritage_attribs, clan_member_info.clan_heritage_attribs))
578      {
579          refresh_clan_attrib = true;
580      }
581  
582      clan_member_info &lt;&lt; data;
583      refresh_union_fields();
584      this.save();
585      this.broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "union_dbase", get_union_data());
586  
587      if(refresh_clan_attrib)
588          this.broadcast_atrrib_changed();
589  
590      if(data.clan_heritage_attribs &amp;&amp; is_master_clan())
591      {
592          catch(upload_union_gang_right_log());
593      }
594  }

630  public map get_union_share_member_data()
631  {
632      if(!is_union())
633          return nil;
634      int max_level = 0;
635      map max_clan_info = {};
636      for(string __clan_rid, map clan_info : get_union_members())
637      {
638          if(clan_info.clan_heritage_total_level &gt; max_level)
639          {
640              max_level = clan_info.clan_heritage_total_level;
641              max_clan_info = clan_info;
642          }
643          else if(max_level != 0 &amp;&amp; clan_info.clan_heritage_total_level == max_level)
644          {
645              int this_house_level = get_house_level(clan_info);
646              int max_house_level = get_house_level(max_clan_info);
647              if(this_house_level &gt; max_house_level)
648              {
649                  max_clan_info = clan_info;
650              }
651              else if(this_house_level == max_house_level &amp;&amp; clan_info.combat_capacity &gt; max_clan_info.combat_capacity)
652              {
653                  max_clan_info = clan_info;
654              }
655          }
656      }
657  
658      return max_clan_info;
659  }
660  
661  // 获取洞府等级加成值
662  int get_house_level(map data)
663  {
664      int ret = 0;
665      if(!data.clan_heritage_attribs || !data.clan_heritage_attribs.normal)
666      {
667          return ret;
668      }
669  
670      for(array info : data.clan_heritage_attribs.normal)
671      {
672          if(info[0] == AFFIX_ASHRAM_HOUSE_MAX_LEVEL)
673          {
674              return info[1]
675          }
676      }
677  
678      return ret;
679  }

691  public void upload_union_gang_right_log(bool force = false)
692  {
693      if(!is_union())
694          return;
695      int upload_clan_heritage_level = this.query("upload_clan_heritage_level") ?? 0;
696      map max_clan_info = get_union_share_member_data();
697      if(is_empty(max_clan_info))
698          return;
699      int max_level = max_clan_info.clan_heritage_total_level;
700      if(max_level &gt; upload_clan_heritage_level || force)
701      {
702          this.set("upload_clan_heritage_level", max_level);
703          this.set_dirty(true);
704          string clan_desc = sprintf("%s:%s", max_clan_info.clan_name, max_clan_info.clan_rid);
705          int house_level = get_house_level(max_clan_info);
706          logex.union_gang_right_log("system", get_union_rid(), get_union_name(), gen_clan_member_log_desc(), max_level, clan_desc, house_level);
707      }
708  }

710  // 同步宗门战力给仙盟
711  public void union_sync_clan_combat_capacity(int combat_capacity)
712  {
713      if(!is_union())
714          return;
715      int last_sync_combat_capacity = this.query_temp("last_sync_combat_capacity") ?? 0;
716      if(last_sync_combat_capacity == combat_capacity)
717          return;
718      // 默认5分钟
719      int union_sync_combat_capacity_interval = UnionD.get_config("union_sync_combat_capacity_interval") ?? 300;
720      int last_sync_combat_capacity_time = this.query_temp("last_sync_combat_capacity_time") ?? 0;
721      int cur_time = time.time();
722      if(cur_time - last_sync_combat_capacity_time &lt; union_sync_combat_capacity_interval)
723          return;
724      // 同步给主仙盟，主仙盟再同步给成员
725      map data = {};
726      data.clan_level = this.get_level();
727      data.combat_capacity = combat_capacity;
728      data.member_count = this.get_member_num();
729      UnionD.sync_clan_member_data(_union_server_id, _union_rid, this.get_rid(), data);

202      if (IS_DEBUG)
203      {
204          object ob = GangSeasonGameD.get_activity_ob();
205          if (ob=&gt;query("gm_union_unlock") == true)
206          {
207              TRACE_INFO("gm放行仙盟操作。。。。。。。");
208              return true;
209          }
210      }
211      int sign_time = GangSeasonTimeD.get_sign_start_time();
212      if (sign_time &lt;= 0)
213          return false;
214  
215      if (time.time() &lt; sign_time)
216          return false;
217  
218      int unlock_enthnic = UnionD.get_config("union_unlock_enthnic");
219      int unlock_time = WorldD.get_enter_time_refresh_time(unlock_enthnic);
220  
221      return unlock_time &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; unlock_time &lt;= sign_time;
222  }
223  
224  // 仙盟操作是否被联赛封锁
225  public bool is_union_op_ban()
226  {
227      // 未开仙盟玩法的赛季不封锁
228      if (!PlayManagerD.is_union_open())
229          return false;
230  
231      // 不在赛季区间内不封锁
232      if (!GangSeasonTimeD.is_in_season())
233          return false;
234  
235      // 报名期开始时服务器还没解锁仙盟，本赛季全程不允许任何仙盟操作
236      if (!is_union_unlock())
237          return true;
238  
239      // 获取本赛季封锁起始时间
240      int ban_time = GangSeasonTimeD.get_union_op_ban_time();
241  
242      // 封锁起始时间小于0，不封锁
243      if (ban_time &lt;= 0)
244          return false;
245  
246      return time.time() &gt;= ban_time;
247  }
248  
249  // endregion 仙盟操作封锁
250  
251  // region 是否可以创建和加入仙盟
252  
253  public array can_create_union(string clan_rid)
254  {
255      // 不在赛季区间内，不可以创建和加入仙盟
256      if (!GangSeasonTimeD.is_in_season())
257          return [false, TT("gangseasonuniond_1607")];
258  
259      if (is_union_op_ban())
260          return [false, nil];
261  
262      // 宗门成员报名是否达标
263      if(!GangSeasonMatchGameD.is_reach_gang_sign_limit(clan_rid))
264      {
265          return [false, TT("gangseasonuniond_10274")];
266      }
267  
268      return [true, nil];
269  }
270  
271  // endregion 是否可以创建和加入仙盟
272  
273  // region 自动结盟上报
274  
275  // do_pre_list 触发：收集本服达标且未结盟的宗门，POST 到活动服。
276  // 候选为空也必须上报，否则活动服永远等不齐。老赛季(未开仙盟玩法)直接返回、不上报。
277  public void report_un_union_clans_local(bool is_report_activity = true)
278  {
279      if (!PlayManagerD.is_union_open())
280          return;
281  
282      // 仅 do_pre_list→do_list 窗口内允许上报；do_list 开始后补跑不再上报（与 GangSeasonVeinD 共用时间口径）
283      if (!GangSeasonTimeD.is_start_match_to_do_list())
284      {
285          TRACE_WARN("Gang Season UnionD: 已过 do_list 窗口，跳过未结盟宗门上报 server_id=%O", SystemD.get_server_id());
286          return;
287      }
288  
289      array clans = [];
290  
291      object ob = GangSeasonGameD.get_activity_ob();
292          if (!ob)
293              return;
294  
295      // 仙盟玩法开启且服务器已解锁仙盟
296      string server_id = SystemD.get_server_id();
297      if (is_union_unlock())
298      {
299          map all_sign_data = ob=&gt;get_all_sign_data() ?? {};
300          array candidate_rids = [];
301          for (string gang_rid, array __sign_list : all_sign_data)
302          {
303              if (GangSeasonMatchGameD.is_reach_gang_sign_limit(gang_rid))
304                  candidate_rids.push_back(gang_rid);
305          }
306  
307          // 字段形状必须与 GmUnionD.rpc_get_un_union_clans 一致（rid/name/server_id/star/combat_capacity），
308          // 供 UnionD.auto_make_union 直接消费；不是 UnionD.get_un_union_clan_list_local 的 clan_rid/clan_name。
309          clans = function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (array candidate_rids) {
310              array clans = [];
311              for (string clan_rid : candidate_rids)
312              {
313                  object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
314                  if (!clan_ob || clan_ob.is_union())
315                      continue;
316  
317                  map clan_info = {};
318                  clan_info.rid = clan_rid;
319                  clan_info.name = clan_ob.get_name();
320                  clan_info.server_id = server_id;
321                  clan_info.star = clan_ob.get_star();
322                  clan_info.combat_capacity = clan_ob.get_combat_capacity();
323                  clans.push_back(clan_info);
324              }
325              return clans;
326          }, candidate_rids);
327      }
328  
329      TRACE_WARN("Gang Season UnionD: 上报未结盟宗门 server_id=%O, num=%O", server_id, clans.length());
330      if (IS_DEBUG)
331          TRACE_INFO("Gang Season UnionD: 上报未结盟宗门 clans=%O", clans);
332  
333      if (is_report_activity)
334          ob.POST_ACTIVITY(GangSeasonConst.REMOTE_OB_REPORT_UN_UNION_CLANS, server_id, clans);
335  }
336  
337  // endregion 自动结盟上报

352  {
353      if (!PlayManagerD.is_union_open())
354      {
355          TRACE_INFO("Gang Season UnionD: not union open");
356          return false;

365      return true;

382  // gm:让本服宗门直接加入指定仙盟(跳过申请/审批,只用于调试)
383  //  clan_info 只给最小定位信息(rid+所在服),完整入盟数据由 UnionD 内部重新生成
384  public void gm_apply_union(string union_rid, string clan_rid)
385  {
386      function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (string union_rid, string clan_rid) {
387          // 入盟必须由仙盟主宗门执行
388          object union_clan_ob = UnionD.get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_rid);
389          if (!union_clan_ob)
390          {
391              printlnf("gm_apply_union: union不存在, union_rid=%O", union_rid);
392              return;
393          }
394  
395          object clan_ob = clan.get_clan(clan_rid);
396          if (!clan_ob)
397          {
398              printlnf("gm_apply_union: 宗门不存在, clan_rid=%O", clan_rid);
399              return;
400          }
401  
402          map clan_info = {
403              "clan_rid" : clan_rid,
404              "clan_server_id" : SystemD.get_server_id(),
405              "clan_star" : clan_ob.get_star(),
406              "clan_name" : clan_ob.get_name(),
407          };
408  
409          map ret = UnionD.clan_join_union(union_clan_ob, clan_info);
410          string leader_rid = union_clan_ob.get_rid();
411          on_union_create_and_join(leader_rid, [clan_rid, leader_rid]);
412          printlnf("gm_apply_union: union_rid=%O, clan_rid=%O, ret=%O", union_rid, clan_rid, ret);
413      }, union_rid, clan_rid);
414  }

416  // endregion gm</message>

 36  public void create_union(object user, string union_name)
 37  {
 38      object clan_ob = user.get_clan();
 39      if(clan_ob)
 40      {
 41          int need_star = UnionD.get_config("union_basic_gang_star");
 42          function.call_in_domain(clan_ob, (){
 43              clan_ob.add_clan_supplies(10000, "gm");
 44              int star = clan_ob.get_star();
 45              if(star &lt; need_star)
 46              {
 47                  clan_ob.set_and_broadcast("star", need_star);
 48                  clan_ob.save();
 49              }
 50          });
 51      }
 52          
 53      map context = {"name":union_name};
 54      map ret = UnionUserD.request_create_union(user, context);
 55      if(ret.ok)
 56          user.show_common_tip("创建仙盟成功");
 57      else
 58          user.show_common_tip(["创建仙盟失败,reason=%s", ret.reason ?? ""]);
 59  }

 62  {

 67      }
 68      else
 69          user.show_common_tip("仙盟已解散");
 70  }

 72  public void join_union(object user, string union_rid, string union_server_id)
 73  {
 74      object clan_ob = user.get_clan();
 75      if(!clan_ob)
 76      {
 77          user.show_common_tip("请先加入宗门");
 78          return;
 79      }
 80      function.call_in_domain(clan_ob, (){
 81          int star = clan_ob.get_star();
 82          if(star &lt;= 0)
 83          {
 84              clan_ob.set_and_broadcast("star", 1);
 85              clan_ob.save();
 86          }
 87          map clan_info = UnionD.gen_union_clan_info(clan_ob);

 93                  user.show_common_tip("加入仙盟成功");
 94              }
 95              else
 96              {
 97                  user.show_common_tip(["加入仙盟失败,reason=%s", ret[0].reason ?? ""]);
 98              }
 99          }
100      });

103  public void set_clan_star(object user, int star)
104  {
105      if (this_domain() != clan.get_clan_domain())
106          return function.call_in_domain(clan.get_clan_domain(), (: set_clan_star :), unpack($&lt;));
107      object clan_ob = clan.get_clan_by_member_rid(user.get_rid());
108      assert(star &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; star &lt;= 3);
109      if(clan_ob.get_star() == star)
110          return;
111      clan_ob.set_and_broadcast("star", star);
112      clan_ob.save();
113      if(clan_ob.is_union())
114      {
115          // 同步给主仙盟，主仙盟再同步给成员
116          string union_server_id = clan_ob.get_union_server_id();
117          string union_rid = clan_ob.get_union_rid();
118          map data = {};
119          data.clan_star = star;
120          data.combat_capacity = clan_ob.query("combat_capacity");
121          data.member_count = clan_ob.get_member_num();
122          UnionD.sync_clan_member_data(union_server_id, union_rid, clan_ob.get_rid(), data);
123      }
124  }

251  public void union_owner(object user)
252  {
253      string user_rid = user.get_rid();
254      map clan_data = user.query("clan");
255      if(!clan_data)
256      {
257          user.show_common_tip("未加入宗门");
258          return;
259      }
260      if(!clan_data.union_rid)
261      {
262          user.show_common_tip("未加入仙盟");
263          return;
264      }
265  
266      object clan_ob = user.get_clan();
267      function.call_in_domain(clan_ob, (){
268  
269          if(clan_ob.get_owner() != user_rid)
270          {
271              if(!clan_ob.appoint_owner(clan_ob.get_owner(), user_rid))
272              {
273                  user.show_common_error_tip("成为宗主失败");
274                  return;
275              }
276          }
277  
278          // 如果当前是主宗门了,已经成功了
279          if(!clan_ob.is_master_clan())
280          {
281              // 延时2秒，然后通知主仙盟传位置给我
282              DelayD.delay_invoke(2, (){
283                  become_union_owner(user_rid, clan_ob);
284              });
285          }
286          else
287          {
288              if(clan_ob.query_union_field("union_owner") == user_rid)
289              {
290                  user.show_common_tip("成为盟主");
291              }
292              else
293              {
294                  user.show_common_error_tip("成为盟主失败");
295              }
296          }
297      });
298  }
299  
300  void become_union_owner(string user_rid, object clan_ob)
301  {
302      object user = RID(user_rid);
303      string union_rid = clan_ob.get_union_rid();
304      string union_server_id = clan_ob.get_union_server_id();
305      mixed ret = rpc_gm_become_union_owner(union_server_id, union_rid, clan_ob.get_rid(), SystemD.get_server_id(), user_rid);
306      if(is_array(ret))
307      {
308          if(ret[0])
309              user.show_common_tip("成为盟主");
310          else
311              user.show_common_error_tip("成为盟主失败");
312      }
313      else
314          user.show_common_error_tip("成为盟主失败");
315  }
316  
317  public array rpc_gm_become_union_owner(string server_id, string union_rid, string clan_rid, string clan_server_id, string user_rid)
318  {
319      RPC_GET_IN_SERVER;
320      return [gm_become_union_owner(union_rid, clan_rid, clan_server_id, user_rid)];
321  }
322  
323  bool gm_become_union_owner(string union_rid, string clan_rid, string clan_server_id, string user_rid)
324  {
325      object clan_ob = UnionD.get_clan_ob_by_union_rid(union_rid);
326      if(!clan_ob)
327          return false;
328      map ret = function.call_in_domain(clan_ob, (){
329          return UnionD.appoint_union_owner(clan_ob, clan_server_id, clan_rid, user_rid);
330      });
331      return ret.ok;
332  }</message>

448          return;
449

451      this.set("clan_supplies", cur_count + count);

456          int reamin_count = cur_count + count;

458      };

466  public bool cost_clan_supplies(int cost_count, string reason = "default")
467  {
468      if (cost_count &lt;= 0)
469          return false;
470  
471      int cur_count = get_clan_supplies();
472      if (cost_count &gt; cur_count)
473      {
474          TRACE_WARN("宗门物资消耗失败，消耗数量超出拥有数量");
475          return false;
476      }
477  
478      this.set("clan_supplies", cur_count - cost_count);
479      broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "clan_supplies", cur_count - cost_count);
480  
481      catch{
482          string clan_rid = this.get_rid();
483          int reamin_count = cur_count - cost_count;
484          logex.clan_supplies_expend_log(clan_rid, this.get_name(), clan_rid, cost_count, cur_count, reamin_count, reason);
485      };
486      return true;
487  }
488  // endregion
489  
490  // 增加成员
491  public mixed add_member(string rid, map member_dbase, bool save_data = true)
492  {
493      DEBUG_PRINT_DEV("%s添加成员%s(%M)", desc(), rid, member_dbase);
494      if(_members[rid])
495          return [false, TT("I18n_ID_36471")];
496  
497      if(is_members_full())
498          return [false, TT("I18n_ID_36472")];
499      if(!member_dbase.name)
500      {
501          // todo:玩家点击加入宗门传过来的名字有可能是nil,要排查一下
502          TRACE_WARN("%s添加成员失败，成员信息名字为空%s(%M)", desc(), rid, member_dbase);
503          return [false, TT("I18n_ID_36473")];
504      }
505  
506      if(is_locked())
507      {
508          return [false, "clan_locked_tip"];
509      }
510  
511      if(is_lock_by_flag("add_member"))
512      {
513          return [false, "clan_locked_tip_1"];
514      }
515  
516      // 报名了宗门联赛后，加入限制
517      object ob = GangSeasonGameD.get_activity_ob();
518      if(GangSeasonGameD.is_on_sign() || (ob &amp;&amp; ob.is_gang_join_ro(get_rid())))
519      {
520          int cur_count = query("union_add_member_count") ?? 0;
521          if(cur_count &gt;= UnionD.get_config("member_add_limit"))
522          {
523              return [false, TT("clanob_88718")];
524          }
525          set("union_add_member_count", cur_count + 1);
526      }
527  
528       // 记录成员加入的时间
529      int join_time = time.time();
530      member_dbase["join_time"] = join_time;
531  
532      _members[rid] = member_dbase;
533  
534      // 广播新成员
535      broadcast_members_changed(rid,member_dbase);
536  
537      // 先刷新一次战力再保存
538      refresh_combat_capacity();
539  
540      clan.insert_clan_member_dict(rid, get_rid(), join_time);
541      
542      if(save_data)
543          save();
544  
545      hook.invoke("ADD_CLAN_MEMBER", "ADD_CLAN_MEMBER", this, rid, _members[rid]);      
546      return [true, ""]
547  }
548  
549  // 剔除成员 rid:被剔除成员 remover:剔除成员者
550  public array remove_member(string rid, string remover = nil, bool save_data = true)

676  // 玩家消耗宗门物资
677  public bool cost_clan_supplies(int cost, string reason = "", map extra_data = nil)
678  {
679      assert(cost &gt;= 0, "扣除必须&gt;=0");
680  
681      string clan_rid = this.get_clan_rid();
682      if (!clan_rid)
683          return false;
684  
685      object clan_ob = clan=&gt;get_clan(clan_rid);
686      if (!clan_ob)
687          return false;
688  
689      bool ret = clan_ob=&gt;cost_clan_supplies(cost, "user_cost");
690      if(ret)
691      {
692          this.add_statistics("cost_clan_supplies", cost);
693          this.send_dict_field_update("statistics", "cost_clan_supplies");
694          hook.invoke("COST_CLAN_SUPPLIES", this);
695      }
696      return ret;
697  }

700  {
701      string clan_rid = this.get_clan_rid();

703          return 0;

706      if (!clan_ob)
707          return 0;
708  
709      return clan_ob=&gt;get_clan_supplies();
710  }

 2  // Created by daiyq 2022/12/3
 3  // 山峰服务入口
 4  // 本模块负责山峰相关逻辑
 5  
 6  // 基本概念
 7  /*
 8  * 山峰是类似帮派的概念
 9  * 玩家可以注册山峰，注销山峰，加入山峰，离开山峰
10  * 本服务只负责山峰的各种增删查改和持久化，不负责具体和游戏逻辑相关的东西
11  */
12  
13  // 本模块负责：
14  // 1.启动重启服务
15  // 2.提供对外的山峰操作相关API
16  // 3.维护所有山峰实例并负责持久化

632  // 禅让宗主
633  public bool appoint_owner(string old_owner, string new_owner, bool add_log = true)
634  {
635      TRACE_INFO("%s任命宗主%s", desc(), new_owner);
636      if(old_owner != query("owner"))
637          return false;
638  
639      if(old_owner == new_owner)
640          return false;
641  
642      map new_owner_data = _members[new_owner];
643      // 检查任命的对象是否存在
644      if (!new_owner_data)
645      {
646          TRACE_WARN("%M任命的宗%M不存在", old_owner, new_owner);
647          return false;
648      }
649  
650      int old_power = new_owner_data.power ?? 0;
651      refresh_member_data(old_owner, {"power" : (int)CLAN_POWER.MEMBER});
652      refresh_member_data(new_owner, {"power" : (int)CLAN_POWER.OWNER});
653      set("owner", new_owner);
654      hook.invoke("APPOINT_CLAN_OWNER", "APPOINT_CLAN_OWNER", this, old_owner, new_owner);
655      //广播所有成员，宗主变化了
656      broadcast_field_changed(nil, nil, "owner", new_owner);
657  
658      hook.invoke("CLAN_POWER_CHANGE", get_rid(), old_owner, CLAN_POWER.OWNER, CLAN_POWER.MEMBER);
659      hook.invoke("CLAN_POWER_CHANGE", get_rid(), new_owner, old_power, CLAN_POWER.OWNER);
660  
661      // 新宗主处理一下盟主职位相关逻辑
662      if(is_union())
663      {
664          int old_owner_union_power = _members[old_owner].union_power ?? (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER;
665          // 新宗主的仙盟当前职位
666          int union_power = new_owner_data.union_power ?? (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER;
667          string new_owner_name = GameUserD.get_user_field(new_owner, "name");
668          // 如果老宗主是仙盟的盟主，需要转移盟主职位
669          if(is_master_clan() &amp;&amp; query_union_field("union_owner") == old_owner)
670          {
671              // 转移盟主
672              set_new_union_owner(new_owner, new_owner_name);
673              refresh_member_data(new_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER});
674              catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", this.get_union_rid(), this.get_union_name(),this.gen_clan_member_log_desc(),
675              new_owner, get_rid(),union_power, (int)UNION_POWER.OWNER, 1));
676          }
677          else
678          {
679              // 新宗主不是副盟主
680              if(new_owner_data.union_power != (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER)
681              {
682                  // 新宗主默认变成仙盟的副盟主
683                  refresh_member_data(new_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER});
684                  catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", this.get_union_rid(), this.get_union_name(),this.gen_clan_member_log_desc(),
685                  new_owner, get_rid(),union_power, (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER, 1));
686              }  
687          }
688          // 同步修改仙盟宗门成员members的clan_owner和clan_owner_name字段
689          this.change_union_clan_owner(new_owner, new_owner_name);
690          // 旧宗主变成仙盟的成员
691          refresh_member_data(old_owner, {"union_power" : (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER});
692          catch(logex.union_duty_change_log("system", this.get_union_rid(), this.get_union_name(),this.gen_clan_member_log_desc(),
693                  old_owner, get_rid(), old_owner_union_power, (int)UNION_POWER.MEMBER, 1));
694  
695          if(union_power == (int)UNION_POWER.VICE_OWNER)
696          {
697               // 通知仙盟重新刷新一次副盟主的数量
698              hook.invoke("CLAN_UNION_VICE_OWNER_CHANGE", this);
699          }
700      }
701      save();
702      return true;
703  }
704  
705  // 刷新宗门战力，为所有成员的战力总和
706  public void refresh_combat_capacity(bool broadcast = false, bool check_state = false)
707  {
708      int total_combat_capacity = 0;
709      for (string rid, mixed member : _members)
710      {
711          if(is_string(member.state))
712          {
713              object user = RID(rid);
714              if(user)
715              {
716                  //更新最新战力和最大战力
717                  member.combat_capacity = user.get_combat_capacity();
718              }
719              else
720              {
721                  // 状态是online，但是玩家不在线,修复一下状态
722                  if(check_state)
723                  {
724                      member.state = time.time();
725                  }
726              }
727          }
728          
729  
730          _member_combat_capacity[rid] = member.combat_capacity;

149  // 通过这个宏来定义函数，使得函数可以同时被客户端和服务端调用，详情见FRPCHandler.gs
150  #define PUBLIC_RPC_FUNCTION(return_type, func_name, ...)\
151                                                  CLIENT_CAN_CALL(func_name)\
152                                                  public ##return_type ##func_name(__VA_ARGS__)
153  // 指定func_name函数可以被客户端远程调用
154  #define CLIENT_CAN_CALL(func_name)\
155                                                  import game.mods.net_util.daemons.ReflectorD;\
156                                                  patch_rpc(this, func_name);
157  // 用patch或unsafe_patch热更了模块，且模块有新增的rpc函数时，可以手动调用下，其他情况无需手动调用
158  #define patch_rpc(any_object, func_name)        ReflectorD.register_or_cache_function(any_object, #func_name);

184  // region RPC 相关宏 --------------------------------------------
185  // NODE: 以下与RPC调用相关的宏都需要依赖 RemoteRpcUtil.gs, 请在使用的脚本头部import
186  // 避免导入太多的依赖, 所有的实现都放在RemoteRpcUtil.gs中
187  
188  #define CUR_SERVER_ID                           RemoteRpcUtil.get_cur_server_id()
189  #define ACTIVITY_SERVER_ID                      RemoteRpcUtil.get_activity_server_id()
190  #define CENTER_SERVER_ID                        RemoteRpcUtil.get_center_server_id()
191  #define MATCH_SERVER_ID                         RemoteRpcUtil.get_match_server_id()
192  
193  // 远端服务的地址
194  #define THIS_OF_SERVER(server_id, file)         RemoteRpcUtil.make_server_address_ex(server_id, file)
195  #define THIS_FILE_OF_SERVER(server_id)          RemoteRpcUtil.make_server_address_ex(server_id, __FILE__)
196  
197  // 扩展版rpc_get可重试3次
198  #define RPC_GET_IN_TARGET_EX(target)            do{string __target = RemoteRpcUtil.check_convert_merge_server_id(target, __FILE__, __FUN__); \
199                                                  if (CUR_SERVER_ID != __target) return RemoteRpcUtil.rpc_get_ex(3, __target, __FILE__, __FUN__, unpack(0));}
200  
201  // 传入服务器id，跨服调用到对应的服务器
202  #define RPC_GET_IN_TARGET(target)               do{string __target = RemoteRpcUtil.check_convert_merge_server_id(target, __FILE__, __FUN__); \
203                                                  if (CUR_SERVER_ID != __target) return RemoteRpcUtil.rpc_get(__target, __FILE__, __FUN__, unpack(0));}
204  #define RPC_POST_IN_TARGET(target)              do{string __target = RemoteRpcUtil.check_convert_merge_server_id(target, __FILE__, __FUN__); \
205                                                  if (CUR_SERVER_ID != __target) return RemoteRpcUtil.rpc_post(__target, __FILE__, __FUN__, unpack(0));}
206  
207  // 相同函数rpc调用，函数第一个参数需要是服务器id
208  #define RPC_GET_IN_SERVER                       RPC_GET_IN_TARGET($1)
209  #define RPC_POST_IN_SERVER                      RPC_POST_IN_TARGET($1)
210  
211  // 活动服调用
212  #define RPC_GET_IN_ACTIVITY_SERVER              RPC_GET_IN_TARGET(ACTIVITY_SERVER_ID)
213  #define RPC_POST_IN_ACTIVITY_SERVER             RPC_POST_IN_TARGET(ACTIVITY_SERVER_ID)
214  
215  // 中心服调用
216  #define RPC_GET_IN_CENTER_SERVER                RPC_GET_IN_TARGET(CENTER_SERVER_ID)
217  #define RPC_POST_IN_CENTER_SERVER               RPC_POST_IN_TARGET(CENTER_SERVER_ID)
218  
219  // 匹配服调用
220  #define RPC_GET_IN_MATCH_SERVER                RPC_GET_IN_TARGET(MATCH_SERVER_ID)
221  #define RPC_POST_IN_MATCH_SERVER               RPC_POST_IN_TARGET(MATCH_SERVER_ID)
222  
223  // post到所有服务器，不会包含自己
224  // NOTE: 之前如果转发列表不包含自身会直接返回，感觉有点隐晦，现在改成不返回了
225  #define RPC_POST_TO_ALL_SERVER(server_ids)      RemoteRpcUtil.rpc_post_multi(server_ids, __FILE__, __FUN__, unpack(0))
226

141  map on_load_xlsx_configs(map result_dict)
142  {
143      map cfg ={};
144      array level_cfgs = result_dict["level_clan.xlsx"];
145      for (map m : level_cfgs)
146      {
147          for (string k, mixed v : m)
148          {
149              if (!cfg[k])
150                  cfg[k] = [];
151              cfg[k].push_back(v);
152          }
153      }
154  
155      // 加载完成
156      _config := cfg;
157  
158      _power_config := result_dict["clan_power.xlsx"];
159  
160      return result_dict;
161  }
